Conclusion: 奖励函数的形式决定了从最优动作中能学习到多少关于动力学的信息：依赖下一状态的奖励信息量最大（通常可完全恢复），状态-动作奖励次之（存在多参数等价族），状态奖励信息量最小。该结果揭示了奖励形式对逆问题可辨识性的影响。

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>


### [202] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了decoder-only语言模型FFN中SwiGLU开放正尾部是否必要。作者引入了MemGLU（源自忆阻分支几何的封闭尾部对照结构），在9M和30M配对预训练中，MemGLU的验证NLL与SwiGLU相差约0.1%。尽管训练好的SwiGLU检查点对正尾部抑制敏感，但机制诊断显示两种模型的门控使用方式不同但损失相似，表明模型会适应预训练时可用的门控几何。结论是：在测试规模下，SwiGLU的开放正尾部对decoder-only语言模型FFN并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SwiGLU激活函数中开放正尾部（open positive tail）在decoder-only语言模型FFN中是否真正必要，通过引入封闭尾部的对照结构MemGLU来进行系统性比较验证。

Method: 引入MemGLU作为封闭尾部对照结构（源自忆阻分支几何），在9M和30M两种规模下进行配对预训练实验（每种规模三个随机种子），对比MemGLU与SwiGLU的验证NLL，并进行机制诊断分析门控使用方式的差异。

Result: MemGLU在验证NLL上与SwiGLU相差约0.1%；训练好的SwiGLU检查点对正尾部抑制敏感；两种模型门控使用方式不同但损失相似。

Conclusion: 模型会适应预训练时可用的门控几何；在测试规模下，SwiGLU的开放正尾部对于decoder-only语言模型FFN并非必要组件。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [203] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态时序图预测的GNN层GLIDE，通过路径消息传递和静态/动态传播分离两大机制，显著提升了高动态环境下多变量时间序列预测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN模型假设静态图拓扑结构，但在实际应用中时序序列之间的成对相关性会随时间剧烈演化。作者发现许多流行模型（包括Transformer）在高动态环境中泛化能力差，甚至不如简单的结构无关基线，因此需要新的架构来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV)指标，用于量化潜在结构的分布演化程度；2) 建立了TCV与性能退化之间的明确联系；3) 提出GLIDE层，包含两个核心设计机制：D1-Path-based Message Passing（基于路径的消息传递，捕获路径级邻域信息）和D2-Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation（静态与动态传播分离，通过局部静态近似识别最优动态）。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，GLIDE在静态和动态场景下平均性能提升高达45.6%，最大提升达到85.7%。

Conclusion: GLIDE通过引入路径级消息传递和静态/动态传播分离机制，能够显著改善动态拓扑下的时序预测性能，同时在静态场景下保持鲁棒性，为动态环境下多元时序图预测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [204] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无回放缓冲区、无目标网络的并行化Q网络（PQN）框架下，CNN视觉编码器的架构设计空间。通过设计并评估8种CNN拓扑，结合Hadamax编码和分布式、集成、决斗等Q-learning增强头，提出复合架构Aftab，在Atari-57上达到IQM人类归一化分数6.479，超越标准PQN基线，并在Procgen Hard基准上验证了分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQN算法虽已实现稳定且高效的无偏策略学习（无需回放缓冲区和目标网络），但其视觉编码器在此无缓冲设置下的表征能力和参数效率尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地优化CNN架构以提升样本效率和参数利用率。

Method: 首先设计了八种不同的CNN拓扑结构，在严格参数约束下评估其样本效率；其次整合Hadamax编码范式，并结合先进的Q-learning扩展（分布式、集成、决斗头部）来增强表征和价值估计能力；最终通过组合优化形成复合架构Aftab，在Atari-57和Procgen Hard基准上进行广泛实验验证。

Result: Aftab在Atari-57上取得IQM人类归一化分数6.479，相比标准PQN基线有0.86的提升概率；在Procgen Hard基准上取得IQM归一化分数0.418，优于基线0.382，验证了良好的分布外泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为无缓冲、并行化的模型无关强化学习建立了高效且概率上更优的结构参考，同时保持了简单性和内存效率。完整框架已开源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [205] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代数化的残差学习方法（FPRC-PQ），将异质表征的残差视为带类型的对象，通过"松弛-聚合-闭合"的有序算子组合来保持或有意擦除表征的身份信息，在3.67M中国A股日度数据上实现了净收益从13.52%到19.10%、夏普比率从1.42到2.09的提升，证明收益并非来自更多特征或更多树，而是来自显式的残差所有权与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习异质表征的方法简化为特征拼接，这会抹掉哪个表征产生了哪个误差的信息。作者希望保留残差的"所有权"（哪个表征产生），通过代数化来显式建模这一信息结构。

Method: 将表征定义为同时拥有坐标系和未解决残差的类型化对象。FPRC-PQ实现为"松弛-聚合-闭合"三阶段：1) 每个场用自己的坐标系和自身残差拟合修正来松弛；2) 修正后的场在固定均值处聚合，这是唯一的身份擦除边界；3) 共享学习器仅闭合聚合体的新残差。同时提出反射性反刍算子，通过正交投影而非网格搜索确定增益。

Result: 在2023-2026年3.67M中国A股日度观测上（冻结时点协议），基础代数将净收益从13.52%提升至19.10%，夏普比率从1.42提升至2.09。匹配容量、统一残差、无身份两阶段、仅成对控制等方法均落后，证明增益来源于显式的残差所有权和组合，而非更多特征或树。

Conclusion: 在表征身份仍可用时，显式维护残差所有权并通过有序算子组合（松弛-聚合-闭合）进行学习，比简单特征拼接更有效。聚合体可作为学习控制变量接口，具有总体方差缩减和耦合路径均值正交性。

Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

</details>


### [206] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniDecVAEs框架，一种用于多模态可穿戴计算的统一、可扩展的全栈表示学习框架。它能够同时处理任务分类、解纠缠可解释表示学习、融合以及异构多模态时间序列的生成建模，在多达30个模态的HAR任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态可穿戴计算方法无法作为全栈可穿戴处理器运作，即不能同时解决任务分类性能、解纠缠可解释表示学习、融合以及高度异构多模态时间序列的生成建模问题。

Method: OmniDecVAEs将DecVAEs扩展为学习模态条件的时频潜在子空间，通过多视图自监督分解损失和共享的非对称自编码器架构，在统一且可扩展的方式下从任意数量的模态中学习多用途表示。

Result: 在多达30个模态的挑战性全模态HAR设置中，相比基于Transformer和VAE的方法，OmniDecVAEs在活动和身份识别上分别提升1.01%和6.75%的准确率；数据重建MAE改善76.84%，分布相似性MMD改善13.85%；模型仅4.1M参数，具有实时延迟。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAEs展示了作为轻量级智能边缘可穿戴设备和临床医疗保健的统一模型的潜力，通过增强的表示能力、模态不变的参数规模和实时延迟，在单一模型中统一了处理需求和能力。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [207] [FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07393)
*Deepank Girish,Yi Hao Chan,Yubin Zheng,Sukrit Gupta,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FedDOSE框架，用于多站点fMRI动态功能连接分析的联邦学习，通过模块化引导的Tucker分解编码高维dFC张量，并利用最优传输重心和Procrustes分析对齐跨站点的类原型，在ASD和ADHD诊断任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点fMRI数据协作训练中，联邦学习面临统计异质性（尤其是站点差异）的挑战；此外，现有FL方法仅使用静态功能连接，忽略了大脑网络的动态信息。这两大问题限制了多站点fMRI分析的性能。

Method: 1) 提出Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition块，高效编码高维dFC张量并捕捉模块级时空模式；2) 在所有站点生成类特定原型，通过最优传输重心（OT barycenter）与Procrustes分析的组合在全局层面进行原型对齐。

Result: 在ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II和ADHD-200三个多站点静息态fMRI数据集上，对ASD和ADHD的诊断实验中，FedDOSE优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedDOSE有效学习了多站点数据的稳健表示，显式分解站点差异并结合动态功能连接信息，为多站点fMRI分析提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过双层优化最大化预测分布熵的方法来改善LLM偏好对齐后的过度自信和校准不良问题，该方法在训练阶段修改模型参数（而非推理阶段的温度缩放），并在多项选择和开放式生成QA任务上展示了更好的校准效果，尤其在域外泛化方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐常使LLM过度自信且校准不佳；传统事后温度缩放依赖特定域，在一个域上拟合的温度无法跨域泛化，因此需要一种在训练阶段就改善校准的方法。

Method: 1) 以最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，直接抑制过度集中的预测；2) 受温度缩放启发，采用双层优化框架：下层用参数化损失训练模型，上层选择损失超参数以最大化熵；3) 为适配LLM规模，采用高效的一阶近似，避免显式二阶计算。

Result: 在多项选择和开放式生成式问答任务中，该方法使LLM校准良好，尤其在域外泛化场景下展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过训练阶段引入熵最大化目标，可以在不依赖域特定的温度缩放情况下实现LLM的良好校准，且具备更强的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），通过端到端终点预测目标替代传统的递归少步预测，将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入并一次性预测终点观测，从而避免递归推断带来的误差累积，显著提升长时域预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型存在根本性错配：训练时使用局部少步预测目标，但部署时却进行递归长时域滚动预测。少步损失优化的是局部转移保真度，而长时域预测依赖误差和梯度在整条轨迹上的传播，导致不同下游影响的转移被统一对待，局部小误差在递归推断中被放大。

Method: 提出DPWM架构：非递归设计，将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入，并在单次前向传播中预测终点观测。这种设计同时避免了预测和梯度传播中的递归展开，使得在自回归展开不稳定的时域上也能进行实用的长时域端到端训练。

Result: 在连续控制和基于像素的基准测试上，DPWM相较递归世界模型基线显著提升了长时域终点预测精度，且预测时域越长增益越大。此外，当递归基线使用相同的长时域终点目标重新训练时也获得了类似的提升，验证了训练目标而非特定骨干架构是长时域预测精度的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 世界模型的训练和评估应在实际使用的时间尺度上进行，将焦点从局部转移建模转向长时域预测精度。训练目标的选择（而非架构选择）才是长时域预测精度的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [210] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）的内部安全机制，发现其安全对齐是稀疏且可跨架构迁移的。作者提出SN-Guided Diffusion框架，利用加权安全神经元损失将扩散过程从安全触发区域引导开，实现全离线黑盒越狱攻击，以极低生成成本达到高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLLMs用迭代并行去噪替代自回归逐词预测，但其内部安全机制尚未被充分理解。理解这些机制的脆弱性对于安全对齐至关重要。

Method: 1) 揭示DLLMs的安全对齐稀疏且可迁移，通过安全神经元映射和剪枝实现迁移攻击；2) 提出SN-Guided Diffusion框架，利用加权安全神经元损失引导扩散过程避开安全触发区域，实现全离线黑盒越狱。

Result: 自剪枝使ASR从2.6%升至73.8%（LLaDA）、从1.9%升至86.6%（Dream）；跨模型迁移攻击ASR最高达86.3%。SN-Guided Diffusion实现AUROC=1.0的提示可分性，对多个目标模型达到最高86.9%的迁移ASR，仅需每条提示20次生成。

Conclusion: DLLMs的安全机制存在可利用的机械性脆弱点，SN-Guided Diffusion证明低成本、高转移性的黑盒越狱是可行的，凸显了扩散式对齐安全面临的严峻挑战。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Progressive Alignment of Recommender Foundation Model through Multi-Phase Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06792)
*Oseong Choi,Hoeinn Kim,Jihoon Lee,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段渐进式后训练框架（LP-FFT-RFT），用于将预训练推荐基础模型适配到下游服务场景。该方法将下游适配与商业指标对齐显式分离：先通过线性探测稳定随机初始化的下游头，再进行全量微调专门化模型，最后通过强化微调用学习到的奖励模型对齐商业目标。离线实验和在线A/B测试均验证了该框架优于单阶段方案和传统非基础模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型通过监督微调（SFT）适配到不同下游服务场景时，优化点击或点赞等任务特定目标，并不一定能与服务策略中对推荐质量起决定性作用的商业指标对齐。需要一种显式分离下游适配与商业指标对齐的方法。

Method: 三阶段渐进式后训练框架：(1) 线性探测（LP）在冻结预训练表示空间中稳定随机初始化的下游头；(2) 全量微调（FFT）联合专门化整个模型以适配目标任务；(3) 强化微调（RFT）利用学习到的奖励模型将模型对齐到实际商业目标。关键设计是训练策略使用稠密隐式反馈，而商业指标监督仅用于奖励建模，而非直接优化服务策略。

Result: 离线实验表明渐进式LP-FFT-RFT框架优于单阶段替代方案，基于奖励的对齐比直接使用奖励模型进行排序能产生更强的服务策略。大规模在线A/B测试显示该框架相比传统非基础模型基线提升了生产推荐质量。

Conclusion: 通过将适配阶段与商业目标对齐阶段显式分离，并采用"稠密反馈训练策略、稀疏商业指标仅用于奖励建模"的设计，可以有效提升推荐基础模型在下游服务中的实际业务表现。

Abstract: Foundation model(FM) for recommendation has shown strong ability to model long-horizon sequential user behavior. In practice, a single pretrained foundation model is often adapted to diverse downstream serving surfaces through Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT). However, optimizing task-specific objectives such as clicks or likes does not necessarily align the serving policy with the business metrics that determine recommendation quality.
  We propose a three-phase progressive post-training framework that explicitly separates downstream adaptation from business-metric alignment. The adaptation stage is decomposed into Linear Probing(LP) and Full Fine-Tuning(FFT): LP first stabilizes randomly initialized downstream heads within a frozen pretrained representation space, and FFT then jointly specializes the full model for the target task. On top of this stabilized policy, Reinforcement Fine-Tuning(RFT) aligns the model with practical business objectives using a learned reward model. Rather than directly optimizing the serving policy on sparse business targets, we train the policy on dense implicit feedback and use business-metric supervision only for reward modeling.
  Offline experiments show that the progressive LP-FFT-RFT framework outperforms single-phase alternatives, and that reward-based alignment yields a stronger serving policy than directly using the reward model itself for ranking. Large-scale online A/B tests further show that the proposed framework improves production recommendation quality over a conventional non-foundation baseline. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/webtoon/rec-fm-progressive-alignment

</details>


### [212] [Rhetorical-Role-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Legal Question Answering over Indian Supreme Court Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06828)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向法律领域的检索增强生成（RAG）框架，用于协助印度最高法院判决的交互式检索与推理。框架采用基于修辞学的分块、融合检索和交叉编码器重排序等技术提升检索相关性，并通过对话历史、查询分类与重写来理解用户意图，同时处理法律文档结构特征（如法官姓名孤立问题）。基于DeepEval框架的评估显示在上下文召回率和答案相关性等指标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的问答任务通常需要大量上下文理解，且法律文档具有独特的结构特征（如法官姓名隔离）会影响检索质量。通用RAG框架无法有效处理这些领域特定问题，需要针对法律场景进行定制化增强，以构建可靠且可解释的法律AI系统。

Method: 提出增强版RAG框架，包含以下核心组件：1) 基于修辞学（rhetorically based）的文档分块策略；2) 融合式检索（fusion-based retrieval）；3) 交叉编码器（cross encoder）重排序；4) 利用对话历史进行查询分类与重写以理解用户意图；5) 针对法律文档结构特征（如法官姓名）的特殊处理。

Result: 使用DeepEval框架进行评估，在上下文召回率（contextual recall）和答案相关性（answer relevancy）等指标上表现出色，证明了该框架在处理高上下文需求的法律问答任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 领域特定的增强策略对于构建可靠且可解释的法律AI系统至关重要，该框架为法律领域RAG应用提供了有效的参考方案。

Abstract: This research paper proposes a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework that is specific to the legal field in order to assist interactive retrieval and reason about judgments from the Supreme Court of India. The solution uses an enhanced version of RAG framework which consists of rhetorically based chunking, fusion-based retrieval, and cross encoder reranking methods to increase the relevancy of the information retrieved. In order to improve conversations, the proposed framework uses chat history along with query classification and rewriting in order to understand user intention from successive queries. Additionally, there are features that take into account structural aspects of legal documents, such as isolated names of judges that could have an impact on retrieval quality. The evaluation was done using the DeepEval framework and demonstrated strong performance on metrics including contextual recall and answer relevancy, which proves that the framework is very effective in dealing with legal question-answering tasks that require a lot of context. The results emphasize the importance of domain specific enhancements in developing legal AI systems that are both reliable and explainable.

</details>


### [213] [BZKO: An Ontology for the Card Index of German Post-War Compensation Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06918)
*Dilek Yargan,Jörg Waitelonis,Mahsa Vafaie,Harald Sack*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BZK本体（BZKO），一个用于德国联邦中央卡索引（Bundeszentralkartei）档案资料语义化表示的两层本体。该本体将基于BFO的领域语义与基于RiC-O、PROV-O和PiCo的互操作扩展结构分离，实现来源可追溯的语义集成，为纳粹迫害受害者赔偿档案的数字人文基础设施整合奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国联邦中央卡索引是记录纳粹迫害受害者及其亲属赔偿申请的关键档案资源，但缺乏语义丰富的表示、集成和复用机制，需要本体来解决这一历史档案数据的语义化挑战。

Method: 提出两层本体架构：第一层基于BFO实现本体论基础的领域语义，第二层结合档案标准（RiC-O、PROV-O、PiCo）用于互操作扩展，兼顾逻辑严谨性、模块化和跨数字人文基础设施的复用性。

Result: 建立了可复用的Wiedergutmachung档案材料语义基础，为知识图谱生成、本体验证以及纳入更多历史实体和不确定时空信息奠定基础；本体已开源发布在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 该工作为将赔偿档案材料整合进数字人文基础设施提供了可复用的语义基础，同时为未来的知识图谱构建、本体验证和扩展保留了空间。

Abstract: The Central Federal Card Index (Bundeszentralkartei) of Germany is a key archival resource documenting compensation claims submitted by victims of National Socialist persecution and their relatives, within the German Wiedergutmachung process. To enable semantically enriched representation, integration, and reuse of this historically significant collection, we present the BZK Ontology (BZKO). We propose a two-layer ontology for historical archival data that separates ontologically grounded domain semantics from interoperability-oriented extension constructs. The approach combines BFO-based realism with archival standards (RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo), enabling provenance-preserving semantic integration, while maintaining logical rigor, modularity, and reuse across digital humanities infrastructures. The proposed approach establishes a reusable semantic foundation for the integration of Wiedergutmachung archival materials into digital humanities infrastructures and lays the groundwork for future knowledge graph generation, ontology validation, and the incorporation of additional historical entities and uncertain temporal and spatial information. The ontology is available on https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bzko.

</details>


### [214] [From Classification to Recommendation: Empirical Analysis of Audio Embedding Models Application for Content-Based Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06928)
*Qingrui Li,Haowei Lou,Chengkai Huang,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种预训练音频编码器在三种音乐推荐系统（基于内容、序列、基于Semantic-ID的生成式推荐系统）中的表现，发现音频-文本对齐和音乐领域表示在直接使用嵌入几何时效果更佳，而交互式序列训练能缩小编码器间差异；同时发现增加Semantic-ID容量不一定提升生成式推荐系统性能，甚至可能引入不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练音频模型大多针对掩码预测、对比学习或音频-文本对齐等目标优化，这些目标不一定能产生适合推荐系统的表示空间。音乐推荐需要捕捉由主观和行为依赖的听众偏好所形成的物品关系，而预训练音频嵌入在生成式推荐系统中的有效性尚未被充分探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 系统评估六种代表性音频编码器在三种音乐推荐系统（基于内容、序列和Semantic-ID生成式推荐）中的效果；并研究残差量化设计（码本宽度、量化深度、保留的Semantic-ID前缀）对推荐相关信息保留的影响；在两个音乐推荐数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 音频-文本对齐和音乐领域表示在直接使用预训练嵌入几何时通常更有效；基于交互的序列训练显著缩小了各编码器之间的性能差异；增加Semantic-ID容量并不一致地提升生成式推荐系统性能，且可能引入显著不稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为现代音乐推荐系统在选择音频编码器和设计音频派生的Semantic-ID方面提供了实用指导，揭示了不同推荐范式下编码器选择策略和Semantic-ID设计原则。

Abstract: Pretrained audio representation models learned from large-scale corpora have achieved strong performance in audio classification and understanding. However, most existing models are optimized for objectives such as masked prediction, contrastive learning, or audio-text alignment, which do not necessarily produce representation spaces well-suited to recommender systems. Unlike classification, music recommender systems must capture item relationships shaped by subjective and behavior-dependent listener preferences. Although pretrained audio embeddings have been explored in conventional recommender systems, their effectiveness in the rapidly emerging paradigm of generative recommender systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically evaluate six representative audio encoders across three types of music recommender systems: content-based, sequential, and Semantic-ID-based generative recommender systems. We further investigate how residual-quantization design, including codebook width, quantization depth, and retained Semantic-ID prefixes, affects the preservation of recommendation-relevant information. Experiments on two music recommendation datasets show that audio-text-aligned and music-domain representations are generally more effective when pretrained embedding geometry is used directly, whereas interaction-based sequential training substantially reduces performance differences among encoders. We also find that increasing Semantic-ID capacity does not consistently improve generative recommender systems and may introduce substantial instability. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting audio encoders and designing audio-derived Semantic IDs for modern music recommender systems.

</details>


### [215] [Hierarchical Quantization with Domain-Adaptive Sparse Routing for Generative Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06997)
*Haiying He,Xiaopeng Li,Yuchen Gu,Kuo Cai,Bo Chen,Jingtong Gao,Yejing Wang,Derong Xu,Ruiming Tang,Guorui Zhou,Han Li,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HD-Rec，一个用于跨域推荐（CDR）的统一生成式推荐框架。HD-Rec采用分层域感知量化器（共享粗粒度码本+自适应路由细粒度码本）构建语义ID，引入域自适应稀疏混合专家模块（持续激活的共享专家+动态选择的专业专家），并设计跨粒度路由一致性目标函数来提升多token物品表征的连贯性。在三个公开跨域推荐基准上超越多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐在单域场景表现优异，但扩展到跨域推荐时面临挑战：统一模型需要处理不同域的异质物品语义和行为模式。现有方法依赖全局共享表征或轻量级域适应，在建模不同语义粒度的异质模式时容量不足。

Method: 1) 分层域感知量化器：共享粗粒度码本捕捉跨域共性，自适应路由细粒度码本捕捉域特有语义；2) 域自适应稀疏混合专家：持续激活的共享专家+动态选择的专业专家，平衡共性与特性建模；3) 跨粒度路由一致性目标：将token级路由决策正则化到item级共识，增强多token物品表征的连贯性。

Result: 在三个公开跨域推荐基准数据集上，HD-Rec一致优于竞争性的序列推荐、生成式推荐和跨域推荐基线方法。

Conclusion: HD-Rec通过分层域感知量化与域自适应稀疏MoE的有效结合，成功解决了生成式跨域推荐中异质语义与行为模式的建模难题，验证了分层粒度建模与一致性正则化的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) represents a promising paradigm that achieves remarkable empirical success by encoding items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs) and modeling user behavior via next-token prediction across diverse recommendation scenarios. Extending this paradigm to cross-domain recommendation is challenging because a unified model must accommodate heterogeneous item semantics and behavioral patterns across domains. Existing methods commonly rely on globally shared representations or lightweight domain adaptation, which may provide insufficient capacity for modeling heterogeneous patterns at different semantic granularities. To address these challenges, we propose HD-Rec, a unified generative framework for cross-domain recommendation. HD-Rec employs a hierarchical domain-aware quantizer that constructs semantic identifiers using globally shared coarse-level codebooks and adaptively routed fine-level codebooks. It further introduces a domain-adaptive sparse mixture-of-experts module that combines a continuously activated shared expert with a dynamically selected specialized expert. To improve the coherence of multi-token item representations, we develop a cross-granularity routing consistency objective that regularizes token-level routing decisions toward their item-level consensus. Experiments on three public cross-domain recommendation benchmarks show that HD-Rec consistently improves over competitive sequential, generative, and cross-domain recommendation baselines.

</details>


### [216] [MISO: Model-Internal-State-Guided Optimization for Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07035)
*Yongzhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Deng,Yifan He,Mengying Sun,Sheng Luo,Yijia Liu,Hao Yan,Zhuo Li,Yi Meng,Huiping Yao,Swathi Hrishikesh,Jing Chen,Dennis Choi,Steven Liu,Lexi Luo,Keyi Chen,Anish Khazane,Marcio Porto,Xiaoya Wang,Emmy Wang,Kangfu Zheng,Xingyuan Wang,Bilal Fadlallah,Gursharan Singh,Prabhakar Goyal,Alireza Vahdatpour,Santanu Kolay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MISO是一个系统工作流，利用模型内部状态（参数、激活、梯度、归一化统计）来指导排序模型的结构优化决策，以可解释的候选编辑替代昂贵的试错过程，并通过循环重提取机制支持自适应优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序模型在既定模型族内反复优化，但选择扩展、替换或淘汰哪些组件通常依赖昂贵的试错实验。现有方法要么依赖人工经验（成本高），要么使用黑盒自动搜索（不透明）。需要一种介于两者之间、既能利用模型内部信息又保持可解释性的优化决策方法。

Method: MISO从训练好的排序模型中提取模型内部状态（参数、激活、梯度和归一化统计），将其聚合成排序、对齐和比较信号，再将信号转化为少量可解释的候选编辑。每次重训练后重新提取MIS，形成自适应优化循环，随数据分布和系统需求变化持续追踪模型行为。

Result: 在广告排序案例研究中，MISO在提升归一化熵指标的同时，显著减少了验证运行次数，优于专家驱动和黑盒扩展工作流。

Conclusion: MISO提供了手动调优与不透明自动搜索之间的实用中间方案，通过利用模型内部状态实现了更高效、可解释且自适应的局部优化决策。

Abstract: Ranking models are repeatedly refined within established model families, yet the choice of which component to scale, replace, or retire is often guided by expensive trial-and-error. We present Model Internal State Optimization (MISO), a systems workflow that uses model internal states (MIS), including parameters, activations, gradients, and normalization statistics, to prioritize such local optimization decisions. MISO extracts MIS from a trained ranking model, aggregates them into ranking, alignment, and comparison signals, and converts those signals into a small set of interpretable candidate edits. Because MIS are re-extracted after each retraining cycle, MISO naturally supports an adaptive optimization workflow that tracks evolving model behavior as data distributions and system requirements shift over time. In an ads ranking case study, MISO improves normalized entropy while requiring substantially fewer validation runs than expert-driven and black-box scaling workflows, offering a practical middle ground between manual tuning and opaque automated search.

</details>


### [217] [Teacher Retains Full Tokens, Student Merges Efficiently: TM20K for E-Commerce Sequence Modeling in Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07055)
*Xinchun Li,Duoru Zheng,Wenlin Zhao,Ziyi Zhou,Jingxuan Tan,Huizhi Yang,Linlan Chen,Dongjian Wang,Dongyue Wang,Xiaosong Li,Hongyue Mao,Yaocheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TM20K框架，通过全transformer建模配合两阶段知识蒸馏，在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统中将超长行为序列扩展到20K长度，在保持训练和推理成本几乎不变的情况下显著提升业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长行为序列建模虽能提升推荐体验，但带来训练效率和推理吞吐的巨大负担。现有方法要么通过搜索或聚类压缩序列损失细粒度信息，要么依赖轻量级目标注意力结构无法充分提取序列特征，需要在效果与效率之间做出权衡。

Method: 采用两阶段知识蒸馏框架：1）教师模型和学生模型均使用全注意力机制而非纯目标序列注意力进行有效的序列扩展；2）学生模型采用多种简单但设计良好的token合并方法大幅压缩序列长度；3）一次性训练的教师模型使用完整序列token进行重度训练，通过知识蒸馏进一步提升学生模型性能。

Result: TM20K已在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统成功部署，将电商序列长度扩展到20K，ADSS提升1.036%，同时训练和部署成本与线上最先进模型几乎持平，服务延迟仅增加5.6%。

Conclusion: 通过全transformer建模与知识蒸馏相结合，TM20K成功在超长序列建模中实现了效果与效率的平衡，证明了该范式在实际工业推荐系统中的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Benefiting from ultra-long behavior sequence modeling, existing recommender systems bring users a better experience via simultaneously considering their long-term and short-term interests. Nevertheless, extended sequence lengths introduce substantial burdens on training efficiency and serving throughput. Prior approaches typically utilize search-based or cluster-based compression on ultra-long sequences at the cost of fine-grained information, or rely on various lightweight target attention structures incapable of sufficient sequential feature extraction. In this paper, we balance the effectiveness and efficiency for ultra-long sequence modeling via full transformer modeling accompanied with a two-stage knowledge distillation framework. First, both teacher and student models take the full attention mechanism rather than pure target-sequence attention for effective sequence scaling. For student models, we propose several simple yet well-motivated token merge approaches, significantly compressing the sequence length while maintaining an acceptable performance. Then, a one-time teacher is heavily trained with full sequence tokens, further boosting the performance of student models via knowledge distillation. The proposed paradigm named TM20K has been successfully deployed in ByteDance's e-commerce advertising recommender system that extends the e-commerce sequence length to 20K, delivering substantial improvements in key business metrics (e.g., ADSS +1.036\%) while keeping the training and serving cost nearly the same as the online state-of-the-art model (e.g., serving latency only +5.6\%).

</details>


### [218] [Invisible to the Machine: Auditing AI Restaurant, Cafe, and Bar Recommendation Against a Complete Market Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07069)
*Vladimir Pitenin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次以人口普查式方法审计AI场地推荐系统，在巴厘岛两个有界市场中对4,776家餐饮场所进行完整枚举，评估2,208个来自四个主流AI系统的搜索结果。发现85.6%的场所从未被推荐，可见性呈现"入榜靠文档、排名靠评分"的双重结构，且系统推荐已关闭场所的"陈旧性"问题远比捏造更为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手正成为本地探索的主要界面，但其推荐的场所选择几乎无人知晓，尤其在餐饮领域推荐直接影响收入。现有研究多采用抽样审计，无法获得完整市场视图，因此无法量化整个市场的推荐覆盖率和系统性偏差。

Method: 采用"人口普查式"审计设计：完整枚举两个有界市场（坎古和乌布）中的4,776家餐饮场所作为黄金标准；对四个AI生产系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、Perplexity）在96个人设条件查询下收集2,208个搜索生成响应，持续7天，遵循预注册协议。

Result: 85.6%的场所从未被任何系统推荐（即使50+评分的成熟场所也有72.6%未被推荐）。入场边际与评分无关（OR 0.89），而与文档化程度相关（自有网站OR 1.92、评分数量OR 1.64）；排名边际则由评分显著预测（OR 1.17）。Foursquare开放POI数据在两个边际均无正向效应。捏造仅0.08%，但推荐永久关闭场所达93次。跨系统一致性低（top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54），时间重测显示波动主要为抽样随机性。

Conclusion: AI场地推荐存在显著的"长尾沉默"现象：绝大多数场所被系统性忽视，且准入机制偏向文档化充分、有自有网站的场所，可能加剧已有在线可见性优势场所的聚拢效应。实践失败模式是信息陈旧而非幻觉，同时跨系统低一致性提示当前推荐缺乏稳定标准。

Abstract: AI assistants are becoming a primary interface for local discovery, yet almost nothing is known about which venues they surface -- especially in food and drink, where recommendations carry direct revenue consequences. We present the first census-denominated audit of AI venue recommendation: a complete enumeration of 4,776 cafes, restaurants, and bars across two bounded markets (Canggu and Ubud, Bali), against which we evaluate 2,208 search-grounded responses from four production AI systems (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Perplexity) to 96 persona-conditioned queries, collected over seven days under a pre-registered protocol. Because we observe the full market, we can measure what sampled audits cannot: 85.6% of venues were never recommended by any system -- 72.6% even among established venues with fifty or more ratings. Visibility follows a two-margin structure. Entry into answers is associated with documentation: review volume (OR 1.64), an own website (OR 1.92), listed price information (OR 1.54), and third-party web mentions (OR 1.44) -- while star rating is null at this margin (OR 0.89). Rank within answers reverses the pattern: among recommended venues, rating significantly predicts first position (OR 1.17). Presence in an open POI dataset (Foursquare), a folk-theorized visibility factor, shows no positive effect at either margin. Outright fabrication is rare (0.08% of mentions), but systems recommended permanently closed venues 93 times -- staleness, not hallucination, is the practical failure mode. Cross-system agreement is low (top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54). A two-week test-retest shows cross-period answer similarity comparable to same-day rerun similarity: the churn is sampling stochasticity, not temporal drift. We release our protocol, registry construction method, and derived data.

</details>


### [219] [Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval Without Fixed Top-L Cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07152)
*Chunran Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EAHR（Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval），一种自适应混合检索方法，通过精确修复由完整列表加权RRF定义的Top-K排名，并将通道深度作为逐请求的执行状态来处理，从而避免预设固定Top-L截断带来的精度损失和执行成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常使用固定的Top-L结果从密集和稀疏检索器融合，将超出部分视为零。但截断融合与完整列表融合并不等价，未读的跨列表排名可能改变Top-K的成员或顺序。此外，由于通道排名随查询和语料库更新而变化，从历史查询中选择的深度可能无法可靠迁移。

Method: EAHR将完整列表加权RRF定义的排序Top-K作为检索目标，将通道深度视为请求特定的执行状态。使用Per-Vector Scalar Quantization (PVS)和Posting Block-Max (PBM)生成可恢复的精确稠密和稀疏排名。融合机制对未读贡献进行上界估计，仅在可能改变Top-K时才请求更多排名。

Result: 在5个测试集和5个时间性语料快照上，完整列表加权RRF保持竞争力，而固定深度不可靠迁移。EAHR在150个查询-快照组合中全部再现了完整列表的有序Top-20。在warm-cache、交错、有序平衡协议下，穷举批处理与EAHR的成对几何平均延迟比在TREC-DL 2019为23.35，TREC-DL 2020为30.28。反相关排序会耗尽两个列表，某些困难查询在EAHR下更慢。

Conclusion: EAHR不保证每个请求都加速；它固定精确结果的同时将执行深度自适应于当前排名。该工作指出固定Top-L深度选择在真实动态场景下存在可靠性问题，而自适应深度策略能在保持精确性的同时显著提升效率。

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems often fuse fixed Top-$L$ results from dense and sparse retrievers, treating later contributions as zero. The cutoff therefore determines both the ranking and its execution cost. Yet truncated fusion is not generally equivalent to complete-list fusion: unread cross-list ranks can change Top-$K$ membership or order even when the observed candidates contain every item in the complete-list Top-$K$. Because channel rankings vary across queries and corpus updates, a depth selected from historical queries may not transfer reliably. We propose Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (EAHR), which fixes the ordered Top-$K$ defined by complete-list weighted RRF as the retrieval target and treats channel depth as request-specific execution state. Per-Vector Scalar Quantization (PVS) and Posting Block-Max (PBM) produce resumable exact dense and sparse rankings. Fusion bounds unread contributions and requests further ranks only while they can change the Top-$K$. Every successful request therefore matches complete-list fusion without a preset Top-$L$; otherwise, execution continues safely to list exhaustion. Across five test collections and five temporal corpus snapshots, complete-list weighted RRF remained competitive, whereas fixed depths selected from historical queries did not transfer reliably. EAHR reproduced the complete-list ordered Top-20 in all 150 query-snapshot combinations. Under a warm-cache, interleaved, order-balanced protocol, the paired geometric-mean latency ratios of exhaustive batch execution to EAHR were 23.35 on TREC-DL 2019 and 30.28 on TREC-DL 2020. Anti-correlated rankings exhausted both lists, and some difficult queries were slower with EAHR. EAHR does not guarantee a speedup for every request; it fixes the exact result while adapting execution depth to the current rankings.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Recipes for Creativity: Iterative Generation and Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07243)
*Rens Anderson,Tessa Verhoef,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本飞行员研究将FunSearch方法适配到食谱生成任务（2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off），通过迭代生成-评估-精炼循环探索是否提升LLM创造力，并以基于TTCT的LLM评估对照人类基准。结果显示迭代生成-选择可产生与人类基准相当的创造力分数，但单纯增加迭代次数并不提升创造力；循环内评估器的选择是最关键因素，较小的选择评分模型在多数TTCT维度上得分更高，温度参数除原创性外影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型通常仅通过单一产物评估，而人类创造力往往体现在迭代生成、评估和精炼的过程中。该研究旨在探究迭代搜索是否能提升LLM的创造力，填补了现有评估方式与人类创造性过程之间的差距。

Method: 将FunSearch（函数搜索方法）适配到食谱生成任务，针对2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off比赛进行。设计了两个实验，分别测试迭代次数、生成器温度以及循环内选择评分器的模型大小这三个变量，并使用基于TTCT（托兰斯创造力测验）的LLM评估框架对输出进行评分，与人类基准对比。

Result: 迭代生成-选择循环能产出创造力分数与人类基准相当的食谱；但增加迭代次数本身并不能提升创造力。循环内评估器是最重要的设计因素，较小的选择评分模型在大多数TTCT维度上显著提高分数；温度对原创性有一定影响，但在其他维度上影响有限。

Conclusion: 在主观创造性搜索任务中，评估器（scorer）的设计是第一阶设计变量，其重要性超过迭代次数和生成温度。这对生成式AI的创造性系统设计具有重要启示：关注评估环节的设计可能比简单增加计算资源更有效。

Abstract: Generative models are often evaluated through singular artifacts, whereas human creativity typically emerges through iterative generation, appraisal, and refinement. This pilot study examines whether iterative search improves LLM creativity by adapting FunSearch to recipe generation for the 2024 Pillsbury Bake-Off and evaluating outputs against human benchmarks using TTCT-based LLM evaluation. Across two experiments, we test iteration count, generator temperature, and in-loop selection-scorer model size. Results show that iterative generation-selection can produce recipes with creativity scores comparable to human benchmarks, but additional iterations alone do not improve creativity. The in-loop evaluator matters most: a smaller selection scorer yields significantly higher scores across most TTCT dimensions, while temperature has limited effects except for originality. These findings suggest that evaluator design is a first-order design variable in subjective creative search.

</details>


### [221] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基表示学习范式将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，并结合语义-结构双通道架构与域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多标签节点分类中仅考虑同域场景，缺乏跨域泛化能力；而现有的图基础模型基于单标签假设，将多标签节点压缩为单一表示点，导致语义纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签。

Method: 1) 提出多语义基表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为多个语义基的自适应组合，增强表示容量；2) 构建语义-结构双通道架构，并结合域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验证明了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基表示学习解决了跨域多标签节点分类中的语义纠缠问题，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [222] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一个基于中文成语的跨概念创造力评估框架，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的接受性创造力（receptive creativity）。该框架通过概念间桥梁路径将目标槽映射到可成像的替代概念，构建了包含184个合成项目和37个人类创作项目的C4-Eval测试集，涵盖五个任务设置共884个案例。实验表明最强的闭源模型准确率仅约50%，开源模型表现显著更低，揭示了当前MLLMs在通过跨概念关系解码创意编码含义方面的明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs的创造性能力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但与传统面向准确性的任务不同，创造力任务缺乏明确目标和奖励信号，难以评估。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，但目前缺乏系统化的评估方法。本文旨在通过成语这一富含跨概念映射的语言形式，构建一个具有明确结构、可批量生成且有确切答案的创造力评估框架。

Method: C4框架包含两部分：(1)编码组件——将目标槽通过手动标注并经第三方审查的跨概念网络中的桥梁路径映射到可成像的替代概念，实现具有明确结构、以桥梁数量和深度为难度指标的批量生成；(2)实例化组件——构建C4-Eval测试集，包含184个合成项目和37个人类创作的跨概念成语图形，每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，共884个主要答案恢复案例。

Result: 在评估的十个MLLMs中，最强的闭源模型主要准确率分别达到50.7%和48.0%，开源模型表现显著较低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥梁提示和解释请求仅带来小幅改进。这些结果揭示了当前MLLMs在通过跨概念关系解码创意编码含义方面存在实质性差距。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的MLLMs在跨概念创造力解码任务上表现有限，最强的闭源模型也仅达到约50%的准确率。通过成语这一特定的跨概念表达形式，C4框架成功揭示了MLLMs在创造性解码方面的短板，并为未来多模态创造性推理研究提供了可扩展的评估基础。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [223] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种专为动态字节补丁设计的混合专家（MoE）架构，用Top-K专家层替代传统密集前馈模块，利用补丁熵作为路由坐标来组织稀疏计算，在保持下游准确率的同时取得最低的每字节比特数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字节级大语言模型虽然通过动态字节补丁提升了分词器无关建模的性能，但所有补丁仍使用相同的密集前馈计算，这种统一计算无法根据补丁的语义和粒度差异自适应地调整模型容量。

Method: 将全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层；每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作量计算；路由器直接从补丁熵中选择专家，以熵和长度共同定义特征空间来调节专家特化。

Result: 在匹配的密集和稀疏基线上，EntropyMoE取得了最低的保留数据的每字节比特数（bits-per-byte），同时保持了可比的下游任务准确率。

Conclusion: 实验证明补丁熵是稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，并将MoE建模从基于分词器的表示扩展到字节级动态补丁表示。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [224] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对文本生成视频（T2V）模型带来的新型纯合成虚假新闻视频检测问题，首次构建了纯合成虚假新闻视频数据集（PS-FNVD），并将其形式化为三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造）。同时提出推理引导的R-T2V框架，结合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹进行检测，在10个主流基线上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V生成模型可从头合成虚假新闻视频，远超旧有的"廉价伪造"（拼接已有素材）威胁。这类视频能紧密匹配虚构叙事，形成"模态对齐陷阱"，使现有检测器失效。然而现有数据集缺乏纯合成虚假新闻视频；直接提示T2V模型生成的样本虽对齐完美，却会将检测退化为单模态捷径，导致语义-视觉退化。因此需要新的任务定义、数据集与检测框架。

Method: 1）将T2V-FNVD形式化为三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造）；2）构建首个纯合成虚假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，包含两类样本：虚构事件+对齐欺骗（Type 1）与真实事件+错误视觉来源（Type 2），以防止模型利用单模态捷径；3）提出推理引导的R-T2V框架，通过条件推理生成训练和监督微调，整合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹来预测三元真实性标签。

Result: 在10个主流基线模型上的大量实验表明，R-T2V取得了最先进的性能，相比第二好的基线，在准确率上领先12.20个百分点，在宏F1上领先8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了纯合成虚假新闻视频检测的空白，通过新任务定义、专门数据集和推理引导框架，有效应对了T2V时代虚假新闻视频的新威胁，证明了融合语义推理与生成痕迹检测的有效性。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于解释稀疏混合专家（MoE）奖励模型的专家行为。不同于现有仅依赖路由权重的解释方法，CoCo通过选择chosen-rejected响应对中贡献差异最大的样本，同时捕捉专家的路由行为和偏好判断行为，从而提供更忠实、连贯且更具专业性的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE奖励模型通过路由将提示发送给专门化专家以提高可解释性，但路由权重只能揭示专家"接收"了哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何"评判"响应。现有基于路由权重的解释只能提供专家行为的部分描述，缺乏对专家偏好判断行为的全面刻画。

Method: 提出Contribution-Contrast (CoCo)响应级解释方法。核心思想是利用chosen-rejected响应对的贡献对比（contribution contrast）来表征专家角色，即选择贡献差异最大的响应对作为专家行为的代表性样本，从而联合捕捉路由行为和偏好判断行为，实现对专家角色的忠实刻画。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo相比基于路由的、基于评分的以及基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法，生成的解释更连贯、更忠实、更具专门性，同时保持有竞争力的奖励建模精度。这是首个对MoE奖励模型解释方法的系统性研究。

Conclusion: CoCo提供了一种更全面、更忠实的MoE奖励模型解释框架，填补了该领域的研究空白。通过同时考虑路由和偏好行为，该方法为理解和分析奖励模型内部机制提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [226] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo提出了一种基于记忆引导的框架，将长文档推理建模为动态证据探索过程，通过三级检索状态（文档模式记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情景记忆）结合贝叶斯页面信念更新与汤普森采样，实现对稀疏异构证据的持续定位，在3个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档理解系统面临两大局限：单轮方法固定top-k页面集，无法从早期检索错误中恢复；迭代方法虽支持多轮证据获取，但未研究跨轮状态的传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: DocMemo维护三级检索状态：文档模式记忆（结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关性估计）和问题情景记忆（查询特定推理轨迹）。推理过程中通过贝叶斯页面信念更新与汤普森采样、空间邻近传播、结构感知自适应粒度证据访问来持续优化跨轮页面选择，并补充细粒度视觉区域证据。

Result: 在3个基准测试上取得SOTA性能，实验验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过将长文档推理形式化为动态证据探索，利用结构化记忆与贝叶斯信念更新机制，有效解决了静态检索和跨轮记忆脆弱的问题，为长文档理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [227] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的无监督社区检测方法，无需训练数据和标签，通过四阶段流水线（局部初始化、多因素合并、多粒度细化、全局选择）实现可解释的社区发现，在真实数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，而深度学习方法虽然性能更好但缺乏可解释性且依赖标签数据和训练过程。LLM具有强大的推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释、无标签的社区检测。

Method: 受自然界相变动力学启发，LUCID设计为四阶段流水线：(1) 局部视图社区初始化，利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因素社区合并，使用LLM诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区细化，并行应用LLM诱导的由粗到细规则减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视图社区选择，基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并且持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID成功将LLM的推理能力与自然系统的相变动力学相结合，实现了无需训练、无需标签且可解释的无监督社区检测，验证了LLM在图分析任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [228] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以"问题为中心"（issue-centric）的智能体优化范式，通过显式维护持久化的问题状态（stable issue identities、生命周期状态、支持证据和干预-结果历史）来指导智能体的全代码自动修复，并实现了ADIAS框架。实验表明该方法在五个交互式基准上平均超越最强基线25.2%，去除核心机制会使性能下降最高40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动智能体设计方法大多以"候选智能体为中心"（candidate-centric），跨轮次的修复经验围绕候选体组织，导致修复进展是隐式的。这带来三个问题：修复定位效率低、部分进展合并缓慢、无效干预在轮次间被传播。作者因此希望将修复进度显式化，以问题为中心组织优化过程。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为"问题为中心的智能体优化"（issue-centric agent optimization），并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两大核心机制：(1) 持久化问题状态（Persistent Issue State）——维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据及干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化（Issue-guided Optimization）——利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，指导后续的聚焦式全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，并在四种骨干模型上取得一致提升。消融实验表明：移除持久化问题状态或以候选中心策略替代问题中心修订，会导致性能下降最高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 将修复进展以显式持久化问题状态的形式前向传递，相比从候选历史中逐轮重推导，能更高效地指导自动智能体设计，显著提升修复效果与稳定性。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAP（中层注意力预测）方法，通过问题对比教师选择（QCTS）识别样本特定的教师层，并将该层注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，在首个语言模型层之前预测视觉token重要性并进行剪枝，仅保留5.56%视觉token即可维持97.5%性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）因处理大量视觉token而存在效率瓶颈。虽然已有研究利用语言模型中层的文本到视觉注意力来指导token剪枝，但存在两个问题：（1）对问题最敏感的注意力层因样本而异，固定层选择次优；（2）获取适当中层注意力本身就需要处理大量视觉token穿过多个语言模型层，已消耗大量计算。

Method: 提出MAP框架：（1）使用问题对比教师选择（QCTS），通过对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力分布，识别样本特定的最优教师层；（2）将该层注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，使其能够从多模态输入特征直接预测视觉token重要性；（3）推理时，结合预测重要性分数与多样性准则，在首个语言模型层之前剪枝视觉token，无需实际注意力图。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上的十个基准测试中，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token即可维持未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本特定的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测，解决了固定中层注意力选择和早期计算浪费的问题，实现了高效且与现有推理加速技术兼容的视觉token剪枝。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [230] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebGrader是一个自进化的程序化评分器，用于大规模语言模型生成网站的强化学习训练。它从自然语言请求中自动推导交互流程（Flow Contracts），在真实浏览器中执行并收集多维证据，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现和筛选可复用的验证技能，解决了传统奖励设计中的成本和延迟问题。在WebGen-Bench上训练8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成完整网站时，强化学习训练受限于奖励设计。手工编写的浏览器脚本虽然可执行但成本高昂，难以覆盖开放式需求；VLM和GUI代理评分器虽然可扩展，但可能在观察到决定性状态之前就给出判定，导致奖励信号不准确。

Method: WebGrader提出自进化程序化评分框架：1) 从每个网站请求自动推导所需交互流程，表示为可执行的Flow Contract；2) 在实时浏览器中物化生成的项目，将目标动作锚定到源码和实时DOM上；3) 沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久状态证据；4) 通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用验证技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结提升的技能图；5) 将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断分离，仅在观察到请求的转换后才给出Pass判定。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的满分并超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: 通过将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断相分离，并采用自进化的技能发现机制，WebGrader有效解决了RL训练中奖励设计的瓶颈，证明程序化评分器可以兼顾可扩展性和判定准确性，为开放式网站生成任务提供了更可靠的训练信号。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [231] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan是一个两阶段学位规划系统：CatalogBrowse无用户信息地重建课程目录（提取优先），DegreeMap仅消费其输出的JSON文档进行超图优化。实验显示96.2%库存召回率、100%硬可行性，完整管线以0.015的效用差距认证99.5%请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学学位规划需先重建课程体系（来自异构源），再优化学生路径。将两阶段耦合会让每个失败模式互相掩盖，因为由规划器驱动爬取永远不会学到当前计划不需要的事实。因此需要强制提取优先的边界，并度量阶段间接口而非假设其正确。

Method: CatalogBrowse：无用户配置文件探索，以低置信度期望边际收益评分合法动作，通过平台适配器确定性解析，辅以span约束的clause-to-AST模型回退，以闭包证书（索引、模式、溯源、引用完整性）终止。输出三份溯源链接JSON。DegreeMap：将这些文档编译为类型化需求超图，用CP-SAT在硬可行性、完成期限、负荷与风险、个性化效用、期权价值上做字典序优化。

Result: 在100所大学宽轨和6所学校密轨上：CatalogBrowse达到96.2%库存召回率和88.7%掩码源恢复率，比穷举爬虫少47%源访问；DegreeMap保持100.0%硬可行性，个性效用比最强基线提升+0.066；完整管线以0.015的效用差距认证99.5%请求（对照特权金标准图）。

Conclusion: KnowPlan证明了解耦提取与规划阶段、明确度量阶段间接口的有效性。通过闭包证书（而非奖励阈值）终止探索，并在类型化超图上做可认证的字典序优化，系统在不牺牲可行性的前提下实现了高召回与高个性化效用，且全过程可验证。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [232] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskSense，一种任务中心的world modeling框架，通过可微分的随机空间注意力机制（由前一隐状态条件化）在执行潜在编码前强制任务相关性，并辅以辅助逆动力学目标来引导注意力聚焦于控制相关区域，仅重建被注意区域而非完整观测，从而在视觉干扰下显著提升鲁棒性和下游控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world models（如DreamerV3）通过重建完整观测来学习紧凑隐状态，但任务相关内容往往只占观测的小部分，背景杂波和干扰物浪费了表征能力。视觉重建与控制目标之间的不匹配导致潜在表征偏向建模任务无关内容，稀释了控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰下严重损害下游性能。

Method: TaskSense框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 可微分随机空间注意力机制，以前一隐状态为条件，在潜在编码前强制任务相关性；(2) 辅助逆动力学目标，引导注意力聚焦控制相关区域；(3) 仅重建被注意区域，解码器进一步以采样注意力图为条件，在随机注意力下实现一致重建。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上与DreamerV3基线保持竞争性能，在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，展现出显著改善的视觉干扰鲁棒性。定性分析确认学习到的注意力在逆动力学监督下能一致地定位控制相关区域并抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense验证了任务中心的世界建模思路：通过注意力机制在编码前筛选任务相关信息并仅重建注意区域，可以有效缓解重建目标与控制目标之间的错配问题，在不牺牲标准环境性能的前提下大幅提升视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [233] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了六个前沿大语言模型在显式引导压力下的行为可塑性，发现模型不仅在引导幅度上存在差异，在响应模式种类上也各不相同，并通过线性探针和干预实验在Llama模型中定位了行为分离的内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型使用不同的数据、目标和安全流程进行训练，但这些差异是否会在显式引导压力下产生可测量的行为差异，目前尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用300对基础和引导条目（覆盖价值观冲突、推理激发和推理抑制三类）评估六个模型，让所有模型作为盲审互评者按固定行为准则分类每个响应，通过留一法共识对24,480个判断进行评分；对Llama模型使用线性探针从残差流解码行为，并通过注入该方向驱动行为变化。

Result: 模型间不仅在引导改变行为的幅度上有差异，在响应模式的种类上也有不同，某些响应模式仅出现在一两个模型中。GPT-5拒绝披露推理过程但保持答案完整（99%对比其他模型0%）。Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。Llama的线性探针在保留集上达到0.87准确率，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动到86%。所有发现均在令牌预算补救和假设盲审对照组中成立。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在引导压力下展现出开发者间可测量的、本质不同的行为特征，且这些差异具有可解释的内部机制，为理解和调控模型行为提供了可操作的途径。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models).
