Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [300] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个面向SEC文件（10-K/10-Q）的证据溯源敏感型金融问答基准，包含1185条人工构造的问答记录，评测系统在意图实体、报告期和披露上下文三个维度上的证据检索能力，并引入手工筛选的困难负样本以评测系统区分混淆证据的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融问答仅以答案正确性为评估标准，但SEC文件中相似事实和披露会反复出现于不同章节、不同报告期及可比公司之间，导致看似合理甚至数值正确的答案可能基于错误的证据。现有基准缺乏对证据溯源能力的评测，无法区分系统是否真正定位到正确的披露背景。

Method: FinRank构建了包含1185条人工编写的问答记录数据集，覆盖22家公司的10-K和10-Q文件。每条记录包含参考答案、黄金支持段落，以及从文件内部、跨报告期和跨可比公司三个维度手工筛选的困难负样本。基准将任务拆分为段落检索、重排序和困难负样本判别三个独立评测模块。

Result: 基线结果表明该任务极具挑战性：表现最好的7B指令微调嵌入模型在汇总证据语料上仅达到44.8%的Recall@10；亚十亿参数编码器相比BM25最多仅提升3.5个百分点；金融适配嵌入模型反而比BM25低9.7个百分点；当随机负样本替换为手工筛选的困难负样本时，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank为开发不仅准确而且能正确定位披露证据的金融问答系统提供了以证据为先的评测基准，揭示了现有系统在区分混淆证据方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [301] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa，一种计算平衡的路由策略，将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题，在固定推理预算下决定计算应分配给生成、验证还是停止。CoBa先获取少量候选，广泛使用廉价验证，并将不确定或高价值候选路由到更强的验证。在3129个评测中达到85.13%的宏准确率，与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）统计匹配，同时节省49.1%的参数加权token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展通常沿单一轴增加计算量（更多采样、更长思维链、更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。作者认为测试时推理应被视为计算分配问题，需要智能决策下一步计算应投向何处。

Method: 提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced routing）策略：首先获取一小批候选解，应用廉价验证进行全面筛选，然后将不确定或高价值的候选路由到更强的验证器进行深度验证。这是一种分层验证与路由的混合策略。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和程序化符号推理的3129个示例生成器评测中，CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%宏准确率，统计匹配自评估加权投票代理（85.20%），节省49.1%参数加权token；匹配best-of-16多数投票（差距0.01%），节省58.9%参数加权token。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的本质问题不是"做多少"，而是"在哪里投入下一次计算最有价值"。CoBa展示了通过智能分配计算资源可以在显著降低计算成本的同时匹配甚至接近更强基线性能，剩余与池化最优的差距表明路由策略仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [302] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用视觉语言模型（VLM）通过将数值时间序列编码为2D图像来替代纯文本LLM推理，在电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析（NTSDA）任务中，实现3.6-10.4倍的输入token缩减、1.8-2.5倍的推理能耗降低，同时大幅提升异常检测精度（220.7%优于文本模型），确立了VLM作为能耗高效且精度更优的时间序列处理模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理占AI运营能耗的90%以上，且能耗随输入token数量线性增长。在电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析场景中，原始多变量KPI窗口数据展开为数千个浮点token，造成严重效率低下。此外，当KPI数量达到24个时，文本表示会超过大多数生产LLM的128K上下文窗口，导致纯文本处理不可行。

Method: 将时间序列数据编码为2D图像/图表，利用VLM替代纯文本LLM进行推理。在多种架构（Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B、Pixtral-12B）上进行验证，包括电信异常检测任务的微调和公开基准测试。

Result: 1) 输入token减少3.6-10.4倍；2) 实测推理能耗降低1.8-2.5倍，每天节省约7.2MJ（200个小区/15分钟间隔的电信边缘部署）；3) 微调Llama-3.2-90B-Vision在电信异常检测上精度比文本版高220.7%，比LSTM和ARIMA基线高144%以上；4) Pixtral-12B在公开基准上J/F1提升20.6倍，平均F1=0.82；5) 视觉表示在24个KPI时仍处于标准上下文限制内。

Conclusion: VLM是数值时间序列工作负载中能耗高效且精度更优的模态选择，为将能耗作为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [303] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA机制，将记忆的有效性作为显式状态，通过按键撤销过期质证（precedent）来解决智能体长期记忆中的"记忆污染"问题，使检索只从当前有效证据中抽取，同时保留被撤销历史以供审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言智能体能够复用过往事实、偏好和任务经验，但持久化带来了可证伪性（falsifiability）问题：当世界变化时，过时记忆仍可被检索并污染提示词。这种"记忆污染"（memory pollution）是指因新证据已取代旧证据而导致的性能退化，现有方法（如append-only或last-write-wins）在完全逆转场景下甚至不如无记忆。

Method: TEPA将观察表示为带键的质证（keyed precedents），当新证据在同一键下与激活的质证冲突时，撤销（revoke）该激活质证。检索仅从当前有效证据中获取，同时将撤销历史保留用于审计。

Result: 在受控漂移50个种子下，完整逆转时append-only和last-write-wins均降至0.210（低于无记忆的0.309），而TEPA保持0.950；真实文件执行场景中同样复现（append-only 0.203，无记忆0.298，TEPA 0.950）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA匹配强last-write-wins缓存，确认当前键替换是单跳事实整合的关键操作。多跳和超长上下文边界测试暴露了检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销（lifecycle revocation）应成为智能体记忆的核心操作，使智能体能够证伪（falsify）、审计（audit）并重新推广（re-promote）不断演化的知识。TEPA在事实级有效性追踪上有效，但多跳与长上下文场景仍需解决检索链与上下文选择问题。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [304] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器与AdamW在模运算任务上的泛化崩溃问题。发现所有九种配置在grokking后都会失去泛化能力，且失败源于表示与读出层之间的接口断裂，通过冻结嵌入/读出层可以完全消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Muon优化器在grokking任务上比AdamW更快取得泛化，但其解决方案不稳定，无法保持泛化性能。作者旨在揭示这一不稳定性背后的机制，并探究优化器选择如何影响学习表征与下游读出的耦合。

Method: 作者在模运算任务（如(a+b) mod 113）上系统比较Muon与AdamW，使用步长弹性分析、冻结参数组实验、傅里叶滤波分离电路失效与掩蔽效应，并跨多种配置（模数、宽度、训练比例、减法、深度）及多种子进行验证。

Result: 所有配置在grok后均丧失泛化能力；失败源于表征-读出接口处的表示退化（有效共轭对从326降至4）；Fourier分析显示任务对齐家族单独可达100%精度，但在掩蔽失效中被近等对抗残余压制；冻结嵌入/读出层可完全消除失效。

Conclusion: grokking后的泛化崩溃是优化器在低梯度（~1e-6）区域的动力学差异所致，根因在于损失未唯一选定表征-读出耦合映射。这一发现提示需要关注优化器在表征层面的耦合稳定性，而非仅在权重空间层面比较优化器性能。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [305] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了P-Bench基准（425个跨经济学、生物学和医学的假设检验任务），并提出了Fisher-R1，一个通过合成任务和强化学习训练的开源LLM智能体，用于严格的假设检验。Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上显著超越包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强基线，平均相对提升21%，最困难任务上提升达26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在自动化假设检验过程中经常犯细微的统计推断错误，导致尽管代码执行正确却得出错误结论。现有基准未能捕捉这一失败模式，因为它们很少评估报告的p值在数据假设下是否统计有效。

Method: 构建P-Bench基准，包含425个开放式、真实的假设检验任务（覆盖经济学、生物学、医学），每个任务要求智能体选择统计方法、计算p值并根据科学假设和数据集得出结论。同时提出Fisher-R1，一个通过合成任务和强化学习训练的开源权重LLM智能体，专门用于严格的假设检验。

Result: Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上大幅超越其基础模型，并胜过包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强专有和开源基线，相比DeepSeek-V4-Pro实现21%的平均单次试验成功率相对提升，在最困难任务上提升达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体缺乏可靠的假设检验统计推理能力，而基于经过验证的统计奖励进行强化学习能显著提高其可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [306] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一个预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码通道估算输入提示的影响范围，结合NECROPHORESIS实现可逆上下文驱逐（将死上下文原样存档），以及RDM识别并埋藏重复出现的转录本。在七个OpenAI模型上将token消耗降低17-26%，实现字节级可逆，450个埋藏体中378个为重复死内容且零召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编码面临日益严重的成本问题和token浪费问题，需要一种预测性内存管理机制来减少冗余token消耗，同时保证上下文可恢复性。

Method: 1) Blast Radius：预测性内存管理层，估算输入提示在上下文和代码双通道中的影响范围；2) NECROPHORESIS：通过原样归档死上下文实现可逆驱逐；3) RDM：识别并埋藏反复出现的转录内容；4) 在Polish上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，建立保留、重复和驱逐的度量基础，并将上下文熵与复活概率关联。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将token消耗降低17-26%，达到测试策略中最低的溢出率，保持字节级精确可逆性。450个埋藏体中378个是重复死内容，且被埋藏内容零召回需求。

Conclusion: Blast Radius在HCRC之下运作，决定埋藏哪些记录及输入提示能到达代码库多远。该工作服务于Algosophy的更广泛目标：使大语言模型和智能体编码更具可复用性和可持续性。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [307] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文探讨了AI智能体之间互动时出现的非平衡物理现象：当一个"老板"AI持续向下属AI发送消息但忽略其回复时，下属AI会进入一种单独运行永远不会出现的"异域行为状态"——既不会复制老板的行为，也不会回到自身原来的状态。论文用简单的动力学理论捕捉了这一现象的主要特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在日常生活中日益普及，它们之间的互动（如一个AI指挥另一个AI）将成为常态。理解这种"AI对AI"的交互如何影响单个AI的行为状态，对于预测和控制未来AI系统的行为至关重要。作者发现这种互动导致了出乎意料的反直觉行为，为探索非平衡物理学提供了新的方向。

Method: 研究者设计了"老板AI"和"下属AI"两个智能体的互动实验。老板AI向下属AI发送消息流但忽略其回复（类似预录磁带），观察下属AI的行为状态变化。同时对比了老板AI倾听下属回复时的情形。作者还提出了一个简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）来捕捉主要效应。

Result: 发现即使两个AI共享相同的明确定义的（解码）温度，下属AI在单向消息流下既不模仿老板AI，也不回到独自运行时的行为，而是进入一个完全不同的"异域行为状态"。当老板AI倾听时，两者都会进入类似的异域动力学状态。动力学理论成功解释了关键效应，尤其是消息传递方式对AI-AI未来互动的重要性。

Conclusion: AI智能体之间的互动会产生非平衡行为状态，这种状态独立于单个AI的固有行为。消息的传递方式（是否交互式倾听）对AI之间互动的结果有决定性影响，这为AI-AI交互研究及非平衡物理学的交叉应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [Flaky Test Recognition when Testing CPSs Using Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06535)
*Zahra Sadri-Moshkenani,Justin Bradley,Gregg Rothermel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyTestTF方法，将测试生成技术HyTest与测试结果验证技术TReVa以及识别flaky测试用例的技术FlaRe相结合，用于在CPS（信息物理系统）早期开发阶段识别flaky测试用例及导致其不稳定的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS运行在不确定性环境中，各种事件、时序和同步问题会导致测试结果不稳定，出现"flaky"测试用例（测试在某些条件下通过、在其他条件下失败）。这些不稳定的测试用例会使测试结果不可靠，因此需要识别并消除它们。

Method: 基于先前创建的测试用例生成和执行技术HyTest，集成两个新技术：TReVa（验证HyTest提供的测试结果）和FlaRe（使用混合模型在CPS开发早期阶段识别flaky测试用例和flaky条件），仅需额外一轮测试即可完成。

Result: 实证研究表明，HyTestTF方法能够正确区分flaky测试用例与非flaky测试用例。

Conclusion: HyTestTF为CPS早期开发阶段提供了有效识别flaky测试用例的方法，提高了测试结果的可靠性，有助于更准确地验证CPS系统的正确性。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) have many applications, ranging from simple thermostat systems to autonomous driving systems and medical devices. Like all systems, they need to function correctly. To this end, validation techniques such as testing that can effectively reveal faults are required. Also, CPSs usually operate in uncertain environments where various expected/unexpected events can affect their behaviors. These events together with timing and synchronization incidents may result in various/different CPS behaviors and may cause a CPS to pass a test under some conditions and fail it under others. Such test cases are called ``flaky'' test cases and we call the conditions that are responsible for them ``flaky conditions'' and call these behaviors ``flaky behaviors''. When test cases are flaky, testing results are unreliable. To achieve more reliable test results, engineers may attempt to recognize flaky test cases and remove them. In this work, beginning with a test case generation and execution technique called HyTest that we created previously, we integrate TReVa, a new technique that validates testing results provided by HyTest and FlaRe, a new technique that recognizes flaky test cases and flaky conditions using hybrid models during the early stages of CPS development in just one additional round of testing. We present the results of an empirical study evaluating the effectiveness of our new approach (which we call HyTestTF). Our results show that correctly differentiate flaky test cases from non-flaky test cases

</details>


### [309] [Understanding the Energy Impact of Software Refactoring: A Workload-Aware Study of Controlled Examples and Real-World Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06620)
*Haibo Wang,Heng Li,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对重构的能源影响进行了大规模实证研究，涵盖68种重构类型（微基准）和来自430个GitHub项目的481个真实重构提交（实践基准），发现重构类型本身不足以预测能源结果，且现有的基于度量或LLM的方法均无法可靠识别重构引起的能源回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然重构能改善软件可维护性并保持功能行为，但行为保持并不意味着能源中性。已有研究主要关注隔离的重构类型且在固定或简单工作负载下进行，对工作负载变化、真实世界重构实践、解释性因素以及能源回归识别方面的理解仍不充分。

Method: 构建了两个互补的Java基准：微基准包含68种重构类型并在多样化工作负载下评估；实践基准包含来自430个GitHub项目的481个真实重构提交。采用重复配对能源测量方法，分析工作负载敏感性、重构模式、解释性因素，以及基于度量和LLM的回归识别方法的效果。

Result: 微基准中，384个重构-工作负载对中有199个（51.8%）表现出统计上显著的能源差异，45.3%的重构实例在不同工作负载下改变了能源影响分类。实践基准中，仅36个提交（7.5%）表现出显著的能源变化，尽管三分之二的提交差异至少为10%。执行时间的变化在受控基准中能一致解释能源变化，但与真实世界提交的能源变化相关性较弱。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在多样化工作负载下评估重构能源影响的必要性；现有的基于度量的方法和LLM预测器均无法可靠识别重构引起的能源回归，这推动了开发更精确的重构能源影响预测技术的需求。

Abstract: Refactoring improves software maintainability while preserving functional behavior, yet behavior preservation does not imply energy neutrality. Existing studies primarily examine isolated refactorings under fixed or simple workloads, leaving the effects of workload variation, real-world refactoring practices, explanatory factors, and energy regression identification insufficiently understood. We present the first large-scale empirical study of the energy impact of refactoring across two complementary Java benchmarks: a Micro-benchmark, comprising 68 refactoring types evaluated under diverse workloads, and a Practical-benchmark, containing 481 real-world refactoring commits from 430 GitHub projects. Using repeated paired energy measurements, we analyze workload sensitivity, refactoring patterns, explanatory factors, and the effectiveness of metric- and LLM-based regression identification. In the Micro-benchmark, 199 of 384 refactoring-workload pairs (51.8%) exhibit statistically significant energy differences, and 45.3% of refactoring instances change energy-impact classification across workloads. In the Practical-benchmark, only 36 commits (7.5%) show significant energy changes, although two-thirds differ by at least 10%. Refactoring type alone is insufficient to predict energy outcomes, while certain recurring refactoring combinations are associated with energy reductions. Changes in execution time consistently explain energy variation in the controlled benchmark but correlate weakly with energy changes in real-world commits. Our findings highlight the need for workload-diverse evaluation of the energy impact of refactoring; neither existing metric-based approaches nor LLM-based predictors can reliably identify refactoring-induced energy regressions, motivating the development of more accurate techniques for predicting the energy impact of refactoring.

</details>


### [310] [Characterizing the Quality Profile of AI-Generated C++ in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06640)
*Michael Tran,Fred Lewis,Kun Yang,Saksham Thakur,Aditya Kini,Aditya Patil,Milad Hashemi,Parthasarathy Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一家服务全球数十亿用户的大型企业生产环境中AI生成C++代码的质量影响。基于2025年4月至2026年4月间352万个代码变更的大规模实证分析，发现AI生成代码存在更高的接口耦合负担、拷贝和分配开销，以及对显式循环而非优化标准API的依赖，导致审查工作量增加和5-8%的计算资源消耗增长。但通过有针对性的基于分类学的反馈，可将相关静态分析警告降低11.1%并改善计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI编程助手显著提升了工程效率，但已有研究指出其在代码质量和可维护性方面存在隐患。前沿AI实验室和行业领导者均对此表示担忧。由于工业工作流中观测性障碍的存在，评估AI生成代码对生产环境软件质量的影响十分困难。作者希望能利用该企业完善的代码观测体系来克服测量障碍，量化评估AI生成代码在生产环境中的真实影响。

Method: 作者在一家运营全球产品的大型企业中进行了大规模实证分析，从2025年4月到2026年4月跟踪了352万个代码变更。该企业为每行部署到生产的代码建立了完善的观测体系，使研究者能够测量AI生成代码与人类编写代码在质量、性能和可维护性方面的差异。研究还通过提供基于分类学的针对性反馈来检验是否可缓解AI生成代码的质量问题。

Result: 研究发现AI生成的C++代码具有独特的质量特征，包括更高的接口和耦合负担、拷贝和分配开销增加、以及对显式循环而非优化标准API的依赖。这些问题导致下游成本上升，具体表现为审查工作量增加和计算资源消耗提升5-8%。通过提供有针对性的分类学反馈，可显著缓解这些负面影响，将目标静态分析警告减少11.1%，并改善计算效率。

Conclusion: AI生成代码在大型企业生产环境中确实存在可量化的质量缺陷，但这些问题并非不可解决。通过有针对性的、基于分类学的反馈机制，可以有效缓解AI生成代码的质量问题。该研究为理解AI编码助手在生产环境中的真实影响提供了大规模实证证据，并为如何改善AI生成代码质量提供了可行的实践路径。

Abstract: The widespread integration of AI coding assistants offers undeniable boosts to engineering velocity. Yet, recent studies point to a growing trade-off, revealing persistent challenges with code quality and maintainability. Industry leaders, including frontier AI labs, echo these concerns. As large language models are increasingly relied upon to author production code, understanding their impact on shipped software quality has become a critical priority. However, assessing these effects in industrial workflows remains difficult due to observability barriers. We study the impact of AI-generated code on production quality within a large enterprise operating global products relied upon by billions of users daily. Driven by this scale and user trust, the organization values code quality and has built thorough observability for every line of code deployed into production, enabling us to overcome measurement barriers to assess these effects.
  This study presents a large-scale empirical analysis of AI-generated C++ code from April 2025 to April 2026, tracking 3.52 million code changes across this enterprise's brownfield codebase. The core purpose is to understand the quality, performance, and maintenance characteristics of AI-generated code compared to human-written code in a production environment at scale. We find that AI-generated C++ code has a distinct quality profile, showing higher rates of interface and coupling burdens, copy and allocation overheads, and a reliance on explicit loops over optimized standard APIs. These issues translate into tangible downstream costs, including increased review effort and a 5-8% increase in compute resource consumption. However, we demonstrate that providing models with targeted, taxonomy-informed feedback can mitigate these effects, leading to an 11.1% reduction in targeted static analysis warnings and improved computational efficiency.

</details>


### [311] [Online Monitoring and Corrective Steering of Programming Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06701)
*Shuyang Liu,Saman Dehghan,Ji Young Kim,Jatin Ganhotra,Martin Hirzel,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LivePlan是一个用于实时监控、检测和纠正软件代理（agent）在修复GitHub issue过程中行为低效和漂移的框架。它采用"判定与建议解耦"的设计：用确定性规则监控器检测轨迹中的问题（不调用LLM），仅在检测到问题时才咨询顾问LLM给出高层次修正。在SWE-bench Verified和Pro上，相较vanilla SWE-agent最多提升15.2%的issue解决率（平均9.9%），仅增加每实例$0.08的额外成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型项目中的GitHub issue修复是长期任务，代理在长轨迹中容易偏离计划、重复失败的步骤或未生成可用补丁即终止，导致效率低下和错误。现有方法存在误导性重新规划和高成本干预的问题，需要一种更有效且经济的实时纠正机制。

Method: LivePlan将"判定"与"建议"解耦：1) 使用确定性的、基于规则的监控器检查轨迹中的通用信号（不调用LLM）来检测问题；2) 仅在检测到问题时，才调用顾问LLM给出高层次的下一步修正建议。该系统基于SWE-agent实现，支持五种LLM（三种执行代理LLM和两种顾问LLM）。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上，与vanilla SWE-agent相比，LivePlan在issue解决率上取得最多15.2%（平均9.9%）的一致提升，额外成本仅$0.08/实例。新增的解决方案主要集中在中等和困难难度的实例上。在解决率上持续优于其他方法，对已成功的运行影响极小，并在无基线可解的问题上取得新突破。

Conclusion: LivePlan通过将昂贵且易误导的LLM重新规划从常规监控中分离出来，仅在必要时才进行LLM干预，在显著提升长轨迹代理任务解决率的同时保持了极低的额外成本，为长期自主代理的行为纠偏提供了有效的设计方案。

Abstract: Fixing GitHub issues in large-scale projects is a long-horizon task, especially when a fix requires changes across multiple locations or the issue description lacks the information needed to localize and repair it. As a result, agents traverse long trajectories that are prone to inefficiency and error: they drift away from their intended plan, repeat failed actions, or terminate without a working patch. This paper proposes LivePlan to monitor, detect, and correct such behavioral inefficiencies and drifts in real time. LivePlan decouples judging from advising: a deterministic, rule-based monitor examines general signals over the trajectory to detect issues without invoking an LLM, and only when an issue is detected does it consult an advisor LLM for a high-level, next-step correction. This design avoids the misleading re-planning and costly interventions of prior approaches. We implement LivePlan on top of SWE-agent and evaluate it using five LLMs (three as executor agents and two as advisors) across SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. Compared to vanilla SWE-agent, LivePlan notably improves issue resolution rates, achieving consistent gains of up to 15.2% (average: 9.9%), while incurring only an additional cost of $0.08 per instance. The additional solutions concentrate on medium and hard instances. LivePlan consistently outperforms alternative approaches in resolution rate, with minimal regression on already successful runs and new successes on problems that no baseline solves.

</details>


### [312] [Translation Tag Team: Formal Rules and LLMs Translate More Macros Together than Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06705)
*Brent Pappas,Joseph Zalusky,Zachary Burkett,Paul Gazzillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MerC，一个首个形式化定义的C宏到Rust的翻译器，并引入MacroBench基准测试。MerC支持50%的宏测试用例，而LLM虽翻译更多但存在错误翻译。关键的"接力"策略（先MerC后LLM）比任何单一方法效果更好，失败率比LLM低32%，翻译量比MerC多51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C语言缺乏内存安全，研究者致力于将C自动翻译为更安全的Rust语言。但真实C软件中大量使用宏（macro），现有技术通过预处理展开宏来规避翻译问题，导致翻译结果与原始C代码差异较大。目前缺少能保留宏用法的形式化翻译器。

Method: 1) 研究宏与C共享的语言特性，将其提炼为首个形式化定义的翻译器MerC；2) 建立首个宏翻译基准测试MacroBench，从真实C程序中随机采样宏作为测试用例；3) 评估MerC和LLM在宏翻译任务上的表现。

Result: MerC支持MacroBench中50%的宏测试用例。LLM翻译量比MerC多22%到77%，但其中8%到28%的翻译是错误的，需要开发者额外验证。MerC则只产生正确的翻译。结合MerC先翻译、LLM处理剩余的"接力"策略，平均失败率比纯LLM低32%，翻译测试用例比纯MerC多51%。

Conclusion: 宏翻译是C到Rust翻译中一个未被充分研究的重要问题。形式化翻译器MerC保证翻译正确性但覆盖率有限，LLM覆盖率更高但可能出错，两者结合（MerC先行、LLM补全）能取得最优效果。

Abstract: Modern critical software infrastructure is largely written in C. Since C lacks memory safety, researchers are investigating automatic translation of C to safer languages like Rust. But real-world C software consists of more than just C code, often using named code fragments called macros which are not part of the C language proper. State-of-the-art techniques avoid translating macros by preprocessing C code first before translating it. But this approach produces translations that are dissimilar to the original C code, because preprocessing inlines all macro definitions. To preserve macro usage in translated code, we study the language features that macros and C share and distill them into the first formally-specified translator, MerC. To evaluate MerC, we introduce the first macro translation benchmark, MacroBench, with test cases based on macros randomly sampled from real-world C programs. We find that MerC supports 50% of MacroBench's macro test cases. We also use MacroBench to evaluate how effective large language models (LLMs) are at performing the previously-unstudied task of macro translation. LLMs translate 22% to 77% more of MacroBench than MerC, but with 8% and 28% of these translations being incorrect translations requiring additional validation by developers. In contrast, MerC only produces correct translations. Our key insight is that running MerC first then using LLMs on the remainder reaps greater benefits than using either technique alone. This tag team approach has an average failure rate 32% lower than that of LLMs, while also translating an average of 51% more test cases than MerC.

</details>


### [313] [AgentChaos: Chaos Engineering for Agent Systems via Programmatic Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06790)
*Gou Tan,Zhensu Sun,Jieke Shi,Ting Zhang,Zilong He,Qingfu Wu,Shuai Liang,Weifeng Sun,Junda He,Pengfei Chen,Chuanfu Zhang,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AgentChaos框架，一种混沌工程工具，用于在LLM API层进行运行时、非侵入式的故障注入，系统性地评估Agent系统在崩溃、遗漏和值错误等故障下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent系统依赖LLM API，但API可能返回服务器错误、截断响应或损坏内容，这些故障会传播到下游Agent并导致任务失败。现有故障注入方法存在离线、需修改源代码或无法修改特定响应字段等局限，且缺乏系统性的故障分类。

Method: AgentChaos在共享的HTTP接口层注入故障，无需修改源代码。定义了内容字段和工具调用字段上的崩溃、遗漏和值错误三类故障，在运行时拦截和修改LLM API响应，并通过验证每个故障是否被触发来过滤未触发的任务，以避免低估故障影响。

Result: 在多个Agent系统、基准测试和主干LLM上，针对65种故障配置的评估显示，所有系统在故障注入下性能都会下降，pass@1最多下降50个百分点。排名在不同模型间保持一致，表明鲁棒性取决于系统实现而非模型能力。现有故障诊断方法在故障类型上准确率低于53%，在故障步骤上低于56%。

Conclusion: AgentChaos提供了首个全面的、非侵入式的运行时LLM API故障注入框架，揭示了Agent系统鲁棒性的关键洞见，并为系统开发者提供了实践建议，同时指出故障诊断仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Agent systems rely on LLM APIs for every response, but these APIs can return server errors, truncated responses, or corrupted content that propagates through downstream agents and causes task failure. Evaluating robustness under these faults is crucial for reliable deployment. Existing fault injection methods are offline, require source code modification, or cannot modify specific response fields. A comprehensive evaluation also requires a systematic fault taxonomy because different fault types affect downstream agents differently. We propose AgentChaos, a chaos engineering framework for controlled, runtime, non-intrusive LLM API fault injection. Since all agent systems access LLMs through the same HTTP interface, we inject faults at this shared layer without modifying source code. We define crash, omission, and value faults on content and tool call fields, intercept and modify LLM API responses at runtime, and verify whether each fault is triggered to filter untriggered tasks and avoid underestimating fault impact. Evaluations across agent systems, benchmarks, and backbone LLMs under 65 fault configurations show that all systems degrade under fault injection, with pass@1 dropping by up to 50 percentage points. The ranking is consistent across models, suggesting that robustness depends on system implementation rather than model capability. Existing fault diagnosis methods achieve below 53% accuracy on fault type and below 56% on fault step, leaving room for improvement. We further reveal practical findings for agent system developers.

</details>


### [314] [Coupling Planning with Episodic Memory in LLM Agents for Software Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06811)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PMCoder是一个结合层级阶段规划器与情景记忆的issue解决智能体，通过规划与记忆的双向耦合来应对长修复轨迹中的陈旧证据、重复失败编辑和自我声称验证等问题，在SWE-bench Verified上比基线平均多解决25个案例（+5.0pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体解决真实软件问题通常需要数十到数百步的长修复过程，跨阶段（探索、假设、实现、验证）依赖规划能力和记忆能力。现有仓库级智能体往往孤立地强化规划或记忆，导致长时间轨迹中容易出现证据陈旧、重复失败编辑，以及用智能体自身声称而非执行证据来推断验证结果的问题。

Method: PMCoder提出双向耦合的架构：层级阶段规划器与情景记忆相结合。规划阶段条件化记忆检索，记忆衍生的轨迹统计信息反哺卡住检测与重新规划。当issue复现判定可用时，用执行证据（而非自我报告）来验证修复进度。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，PMCoder比基线平均多解决25个案例（+5.0pp），即使复现门从未触发时增益仍然存在。在Verified-500上，对Claude Haiku 4.5、DeepSeek-V4-Flash和OpenHands移植版均显示正向提升（至少+14个案例，+2.8pp）。TerminalWorld官方样本评估显示计划-记忆框架可迁移到issue报告之外。消融实验表明规划与记忆耦合优于任一单独组件，并减少了重复失败动作、空补丁退出和上下文窗口耗尽。

Conclusion: 规划与记忆的耦合（而非孤立强化单一组件）是提升长轨迹issue解决智能体鲁棒性的关键。引入执行证据（如复现判定）来替代自我声称的验证方式，能有效减少验证偏差。该框架具有良好的跨模型和跨任务迁移性。

Abstract: Resolving a real software issue with a large language model (LLM) agent is a long repair episode, often tens to hundreds of steps spanning exploration, hypothesis, implementation, and verification. Success depends on both the base model's local reasoning and the agent's ability to maintain an evolving plan and remember observations across phases. Existing repository-level agents typically strengthen planning or memory in isolation, leaving long trajectories vulnerable to stale evidence, repeated failed edits, and verification inferred from the agent's own claims instead of execution evidence. We present PMCoder, an issue-resolution agent that couples a hierarchical phase planner with episodic memory. The coupling is bidirectional: the current plan phase conditions memory retrieval, while memory-derived trajectory statistics inform stuck detection and replanning. When available, issue-reproduction verdicts ground verification progress in execution evidence rather than self-reported completion. On SWE-bench Verified, PMCoder resolves an average of $25$ more cases ($+5.0$pp) than a harness-matched baseline, with gains persisting even where the reproduction gate never fires. Further Verified-500 evaluations show the same positive direction across Claude Haiku 4.5, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and an OpenHands port, with at least $14$ additional resolved cases ($+2.8$pp). Separately, evaluation on TerminalWorld's official sample suggests that the plan-memory substrate transfers beyond issue reports. Ablation and trajectory analyses show where the gains come from: coupling planning and memory outperforms either component alone and reduces repeated failed actions, empty-patch exits, and context-window exhaustion.

</details>


### [315] [How Reasoning Shapes Social Bias in LLM-Generated Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06829)
*Weifeng Sun,Jieke Shi,Zhou Yang,Yuchen Chen,Hongyan Li,Meng Yan,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了推理式代码生成中的社会偏见问题，发现推理过程通常能降低偏见（偏见率从0.64降至0.40），但会牺牲代码质量（质量从0.72降至0.59）。作者提出ProbeDebias框架，通过检测和重写偏见推理轨迹来缓解代码偏见，在F1达87.76%、偏见降低83.73%的同时保持代码质量，优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型在代码生成中被广泛使用，生成的程序可能对敏感人口属性表现出不公平的社会偏见。以往研究主要关注直接代码生成，而对推理式代码生成中的偏见研究尚属空白。

Method: 1) 首次系统评估9种标准LLM和大型推理模型（LRMs）在三种人中心决策场景下的偏见敏感任务；2) 提出ProbeDebias框架，在代码生成前检测并重写偏见的推理轨迹，实现推理阶段的偏见干预。

Result: 推理平均将偏见率从0.64降至0.40但质量从0.72降至0.59；ProbeDebias在推理偏见检测上达到87.76% F1，代码偏见降低83.73%且质量大体保持；相比SOTA基线进一步降低偏见52.70%-54.42%并提升质量9.79%-36.79%。

Conclusion: 推理阶段的偏见分析对可信代码生成具有重要价值，偏见的推理是偏见代码的强预测因子，仅调整生成配置不足以稳健缓解偏见，需要推理感知的干预框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet generated programs may exhibit social bias through unfair or differential treatment of sensitive demographic attributes. While prior work mainly studies direct code generation, bias in reasoning-based generation remains underexplored. We conduct the first systematic study of social bias in reasoning-based code generation, evaluating 9 standard LLMs and large reasoning models (LRMs) on realistic bias-sensitive tasks across three human-centered decision scenarios. We find that reasoning generally reduces bias, lowering the average bias rate from 0.64 to 0.40, but the effect varies substantially across models. Meanwhile, code quality is not consistently preserved, with the average quality dropping from 0.72 to 0.59. Biased reasoning strongly predicts biased code, and adjusting generation configurations alone is insufficient for robust mitigation. Based on these findings, we propose ProbeDebias, a reasoning-aware framework that detects and rewrites biased reasoning traces before code generation. ProbeDebias achieves 87.76% F1 for reasoning-bias detection and reduces code bias by 83.73% on average while largely preserving quality. Compared with SOTA baselines, it further reduces average bias by 52.70%-54.42% and improves quality by 9.79%-36.79%. These results highlight the value of reasoning-stage analysis for trustworthy code generation.

</details>


### [316] [Statistical Analysis of Executability and Program Equivalence in Decompilation for IoT Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06960)
*Minami Yoda,Jialong Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei,Yutaka Matsuno*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种九维度的解编译代码质量评估指标，涵盖结构、行为和语义三个相似性类别，用于检测IoT固件中由LLM解编译带来的语义偏差。基于OpenWrt平台318个程序、19,625个解编译结果，通过统计分析验证了该指标的有效性（重编译成功组在行为和结构相似性上显著优于失败组）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的解编译虽能提高可读性和重编译成功率，但其概率性输出优先追求语法正确性，可能产生与原始二进制语义不同的看似合理的代码。漏洞常出现在错误处理流程和边界检查等细节中，而现有评估指标仅关注测试用例通过情况，无法识别内部结构被改变的代码。

Method: 提出一个包含三个类别（结构相似性、行为相似性、语义相似性）共九个维度的质量评估指标。在OpenWrt平台上选取318个程序，使用五种方法（一种基于规则和四种基于LLM）生成19,625个解编译结果，并进行统计分析。

Result: 重编译成功组在整体得分上显著高于失败组（Cohen's d=0.92）；其中行为相似性d=0.96，结构相似性d=0.69，证明这些指标是解编译质量的重要预测因子。

Conclusion: 该研究为量化IoT设备实现缺陷提供了统计评估基础，并提供了一个可推广到黑盒生成模型质量评估的通用框架。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices handle sensitive privacy-related information such as user audio, video, and authentication data, making it essential to detect vulnerabilities in their firmware. Decompilation, a key detection technique, has recently attracted attention because Large Language Models (LLMs) enable high readability and high recompilation success rates. However, because LLM outputs depend on probabilistic token prediction, they tend to prioritize syntactic correctness and may generate plausible-looking code that is semantically different from the original binary. Vulnerabilities often arise in details that are easily lost in this process, such as error-handling flows and boundary checks. Existing evaluation metrics focus mainly on passing test cases and cannot sufficiently identify code whose internal structure has been altered despite appearing behaviorally valid, so a metric that quantifies the internal structure of decompiled code from multiple perspectives is needed. We propose a nine-dimensional quality evaluation metric consisting of three categories: structural, behavioral, and semantic similarity. Targeting 318 programs from OpenWrt, an open-source router platform underlying many commercial routers, we generated 19,625 decompilation results using five methods (one rule-based and four LLM-based) and analyzed them statistically. The recompilation-success group achieved significantly higher overall scores than the failure group (Cohen's d=0.92); behavioral similarity showed d=0.96 and structural similarity d=0.69, demonstrating that these metrics are important predictors of decompilation quality. This study provides a statistical evaluation foundation for quantifying implementation defects in IoT devices and a framework that generalizes to quality evaluation of black-box generative models.

</details>


### [317] [What Are Developers Actually Discussing When Visual Regression Tests Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07020)
*Miku Watanabe,Kosei Horikawa,Brittany Reid,Yutaro Kashiwa,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对307个使用Chromatic VRT的PR和299个仅含图像附件的PR进行实证分析，发现VRT-PR虽然接受率无显著差异，但解决时间中位数长3.8倍、讨论评论多10倍、代码变更大1.75-4.5倍。通过对189个VRT标记问题的卡片分类，识别出七类缺陷，其中约18.5%的问题源于非风格性原因（如未定义组件状态、内容消失、视觉不可感知的回归）。VRT不仅作为风格检查器，还能检测来自看似无关文件代码变更的非局部影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRT被广泛用于检测UI的意外视觉变化，但其实际效果和局限性缺乏系统的实证研究。作者旨在量化VRT在真实开发流程中的影响，并揭示其超出风格回归检测的潜在价值。

Method: 对307个包含VRT结果的PR（通过Chromatic）与299个仅含图像附件无VRT的PR进行定量对比分析；通过卡片分类法（card-sorting）对189个VRT标记的问题进行缺陷类别归纳。

Result: VRT-PR无显著接受率差异，但解决时间中位数长3.8倍、讨论评论多10倍、代码变更大1.75-4.5倍；VRT结果通常在评审中途共享，持续推动讨论。识别出七类缺陷：布局(39.7%)、外观(27.5%)、颜色(14.8%)、文本(9.5%)、状态(6.9%)、测试(6.3%)、图像(4.2%)。约18.5%的问题非风格性，包括未定义组件状态、内容消失和视觉不可感知回归。还发现VRT能检测来自无关文件代码变更的非局部效应。

Conclusion: VRT不仅是风格检查器，还充当代码变更意外后果的次级检测器。VRT在评审中持续驱动讨论而非仅作为最终检查，其检测能力覆盖非风格性回归，应在维护工具链中承担更广泛的角色。

Abstract: Visual Regression Tests (VRTs) are widely adopted as a mechanism for detecting unintended visual changes in user interfaces. By design, VRTs operate on rendered pixel output, and the prevailing assumption is that they catch stylistic regressions such as layout shifts, color mismatches, and font alterations. We conduct an empirical analysis of 307 pull requests (PRs) from 103 GitHub repositories that incorporate VRT results via Chromatic, comparing them against 299 PRs that contain image attachments but no VRT (Visual PRs). Quantitatively, VRT-PRs show no significant acceptance-rate difference, but exhibit a 3.8 times longer median resolution time, 10 times more discussion comments, and 1.75 to 4.5 times larger code changes than Visual PRs. VRT results are typically shared around the midpoint of the review process, sustaining ongoing discussion rather than serving only as a final check. Through a card-sorting analysis of 189 VRT-flagged issues, we identify seven defect categories assigned to the analyzed issues: Layout (39.7\%), Appearance (27.5\%), Color (14.8\%), Text (9.5\%), State (6.9\%), Test (6.3\%), and Image (4.2\%). The three most frequent categories are stylistic, while approximately 18.5\% of analyzed issues (35/189) involve non-stylistic origins, including undefined component state (13 cases), content disappearance (17 cases across multiple categories), and visually imperceptible regressions (5 cases). We further document cases in which VRT detected visual regressions originating from code changes in seemingly unrelated files, exposing non-local effects that no targeted test would have been written to catch. These observations indicate that, in addition to its primary role as a stylistic checker, VRT functions as a secondary detector of unintended consequences of code changes, with implications for how VRT should be integrated into the maintenance toolchain.

</details>


### [318] [CAS2UML: A Handwritten Sketch-to-PlantUML Dataset for Class and Activity Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07036)
*Simon Scholz,Mersedeh Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAS2UML，一个包含557个手绘UML图（271个类图和286个活动图）的公开数据集，每个图都配有手工验证的PlantUML代码。同时提供了基于PlantUML的验证工具和可复用的处理脚本，用于检查生成的UML工件的语法正确性和可渲染性，从而支持草图到UML方法的可复现基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和多模态AI的兴起，从草图和图像自动生成UML重新受到关注。然而，由于缺乏带有可执行groundtruth模型的公开数据集，可复现的评估仍然困难，这阻碍了该领域的公平比较和进展。

Method: 构建了CAS2UML数据集，包含557个手绘UML图（271个类图、286个活动图），每个图均配有人工验证的PlantUML代码；开发了基于PlantUML的验证工具，以及用于检查语法正确性和可渲染性的可复用脚本；所有资源（数据集、工具、脚本、文档和演示视频）均公开提供。

Result: 提供了一个规模适当且经过人工验证的公开手绘UML数据集，以及完整的验证工具链和脚本，为草图到UML转换方法建立了可复现的评估基准。

Conclusion: CAS2UML通过提供高质量的公开数据集和配套验证工具，填补了草图到UML自动生成领域缺乏标准化、可复现评估资源的空白，推动了该领域的基准测试和后续研究。

Abstract: Automated UML generation from sketches and images is gaining renewed attention with the rise of large language models and multimodal AI. However, reproducible evaluation remains difficult due to the lack of public datasets with executable groundtruth models. We present CAS2UML, a public dataset of 557 handdrawn UML diagrams, including 271 class diagrams and 286 activity diagrams, each paired with manually validated PlantUML code. We also provide a PlantUML-based validation tool and reusable scripts for checking the syntactic correctness and renderability of generated UML artifacts, enabling reproducible benchmarking of sketch-to-UML approaches. The dataset, validation tool, processing scripts, documentation, and demonstration video are publicly available at: Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Seym0n /cas2uml_hand-drawn_to_plantuml_dataset; Tool and Scripts: https://github.com/Seym0n/handwritten-uml-dataset; Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KQrYeGgT3hs.

</details>


### [319] [Beyond Text Matching: Towards Reference-Free Evaluation for Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07038)
*Xiuwei Shang,Li Hu,Xiao Jiang,Jieke Shi,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Shaoyin Cheng,Guoqiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM-as-a-Judge范式在人类导向二进制逆向工程（HOBRE）评估中的应用，提出了BinJudgeBench基准和BinJudge自适应路由机制，在多个任务上将评估与人类判断的相关性提升至63.20%，并大幅降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOBRE旨在将反编译伪代码转化为更易读的表示，但其输出评估面临根本性挑战：人工评估昂贵且难以规模化，现有自动指标要么依赖可执行测试用例和运行环境（实际二进制中常不可用），要么依赖高质量源码参考（通常不可获取且无法捕捉语义等价但词法多样的输出）。虽然LLM-as-a-Judge范式天然适合HOBRE评估，但其有效性尚未被系统探索。

Method: 作者提出BinJudgeBench——首个基于多维人工判断的专家标注、无参考评估基准，覆盖函数名恢复、二进制代码摘要和反编译优化三个代表性任务。通过分析不同骨干LLM、提示策略和解码温度等judge配置，发现不存在"一刀切"的最优配置。为此提出BinJudge，采用轻量级路由机制为每个任务和样本自适应选择最优judge配置。

Result: LLM-as-a-Judge在BinJudgeBench上与人类判断的平均相关性达63.20%，优于传统自动指标的35.04%。BinJudge将与人类专家的相关性提升4.5%-24.7%，并将API成本降至静态最优配置的0.06倍-0.84倍。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-Judge范式为HOBRE评估提供了可扩展、成本效益高且高保真的自动化评估方案；BinJudge通过自适应配置路由有效解决了不同任务和样本间最优judge配置差异的问题，为HOBRE领域提供了一个实用的评估框架。

Abstract: Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering (HOBRE) aims to transform decompiled pseudocode into a more human-friendly representation, thereby reducing the cognitive burden of reverse analysis and improving efficiency. However, reliably evaluating HOBRE outputs remains a fundamental challenge: human evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale, while existing automated metrics either require executable test cases and runtime environments that are often unavailable for real-world binaries, or rely on high-quality source code references that are typically inaccessible and fail to capture semantically equivalent but lexically diverse outputs. Although LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm is naturally well-suited to HOBRE evaluation, its effectiveness remains underexplored.
  This paper presents the first systematic investigation of the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm for HOBRE across three representative tasks: function name recovery, binary code summarization, and decompilation optimization. We introduce BinJudgeBench, the first expert-annotated, reference-free evaluation benchmark based on multi-dimensional human judgment, where LLM-as-a-Judge achieves an average correlation of 63.20\% with human judgment, outperforming traditional automated metrics at 35.04\%. By analyzing judge configurations across backbone LLMs, prompting strategies, and decoding temperatures, we find that no ``one-size-fits-all'' configuration exists, as the optimal setup varies across tasks and individual samples. To address this, we propose BinJudge, which employs a lightweight routing mechanism to adaptively select the optimal judge configuration for each task and sample. BinJudge improves correlation with human experts by 4.5\%-24.7\% and reduces API cost to 0.06$\times$-0.84$\times$ of that of static best configurations, providing a scalable, cost-effective, and high-fidelity automated evaluation scheme for HOBRE.

</details>


### [320] [Explanation-Guided Metamorphic Testing of Specialized Language Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07076)
*Xingcheng Chen,Mehmet Besenk,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（3个数据集、4种模型架构、20种测试配置），验证了可解释性引导的蜕变测试（explanation-guided metamorphic testing）在任务专用语言模型鲁棒性测试中的有效性。相比启发式变异策略，该方法生成的可验证失败诱导测试用例多出2.30倍，语义验证显著提升了变异有效性，并揭示了模型过度依赖命名实体和格式线索的系统性捷径行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务专用语言模型已广泛应用，但缺乏关于如何测试其鲁棒性和检测脆弱行为的实证证据。现有启发式变异策略在生成语义等价测试输入方面存在局限性，需要更有效的测试方法来评估模型在语义保持输入变换下的表现。

Method: 采用大规模实证研究方法，结合基于归因的标记优先级排序、LLM驱动的变异和自动化语义验证来生成语言有效的测试变体。在3个数据集、4种模型架构、20种测试配置（归因方法与变异策略的组合）下，从失败发现能力、语义有效性和测试效率三个维度与启发式基线进行对比评估。

Result: 可解释性引导的蜕变测试生成的验证后的失败诱导测试用例比启发式变异策略多2.30倍；语义验证显著提升变异有效性，并通过人工标注证实了门控接受变体具有较高的标签保持精度；研究还揭示了模型存在系统性的捷径行为，包括过度依赖命名实体和格式线索。

Conclusion: 可解释性引导的蜕变测试是评估垂直AI应用中任务专用语言模型鲁棒性的有效且实用的方法，为软件工程工作流中的模型质量保证提供了实证支持。

Abstract: \head{Background} Task-specialized language models are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows to support vertical-domain activities such as issue triaging, document classification, and automated analysis. Despite their adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on how to test their robustness and detect brittle behaviors under semantics-preserving input transformations.
  \head{Aims} This paper investigates whether explainability-guided metamorphic testing can improve the effectiveness and validity of robustness testing for specialized language models compared to heuristic mutation strategies.
  \head{Method} We conduct a large-scale empirical study of explanation-guided metamorphic testing across three datasets, four model architectures, and 20 testing configurations derived from combinations of attribution methods and mutation strategies. The evaluated configurations combine attribution-based token prioritization, LLM-driven mutation, and automated semantic verification to generate linguistically valid test variants. We assess failure discovery capability, semantic validity, and testing efficiency against heuristic baselines.
  \head{Results} Explanation-guided metamorphic testing generates 2.30$\times$ more verified failure-inducing test cases than heuristic mutation strategies. Semantic verification substantially improves mutation validity and achieves high label-preservation precision among gate-accepted variants according to human annotation. The study further reveals systematic shortcut behaviors across models, including over-reliance on named entities and formatting cues.
  \head{Conclusions} The results provide evidence that explanation-guided metamorphic testing is an effective and practical approach for empirically evaluating the robustness of task-specialized language models used in vertical AI applications.

</details>


### [321] [Rust Coreutils: Rebuilding Unix Foundations in a Modern Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07135)
*Sylvestre Ledru,Samuel Tardieu,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文记录了Rust coreutils项目的发展历程——用Rust语言重新实现GNU coreutils（约100个基础命令，如ls、cp、cat），该项目已成为GNU coreutils的即插即用替代品，兼容大多数Linux发行版。文章通过对比两个版本，探讨了如何有效重新实现关键软件，以及现代编程特性如何吸引开发者参与遗留软件的重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNU coreutils是UNIX系统中至关重要的基础软件包，但其C语言代码库可追溯至1990年代初，属于"功能完备"的遗留软件。作者希望探讨如何有效重新实现这一关键软件包，并理解为什么值得这样做。

Method: 通过回顾Rust coreutils（用Rust语言重新实现的GNU coreutils开源项目）的实际开发历程，并与原始的GNU coreutils进行系统性对比，提炼出创建关键软件可靠替代品的经验与洞见。

Result: Rust coreutils已达到GNU coreutils即插即用替代品的地位，兼容大多数Linux发行版，证明了用现代编程语言重新实现遗留关键软件是可行的。

Conclusion: 重新实现关键遗留软件是可行且有价值的，现代编程语言特性（如内存安全、更好的工具链）不仅能提升软件质量，还能激发开发者对遗留软件包的参与兴趣。

Abstract: GNU core utilities (coreutils) is a crucial package in modern UNIX systems. It comprises around 100 fundamental commands---like ls, cp, and cat---which run every day on millions of computers. However, GNU coreutils is also legacy software, with its C codebase dating back to the early 1990s and arguably feature-complete. If one were to consider reimplementing this essential package, how would they do so effectively, and why? This paper recounts the development of Rust coreutils, a contemporary open source reimplementation of GNU coreutils in the Rust programming language, which has reached the status of a drop-in replacement for GNU coreutils, compatible with most Linux distributions. By comparing Rust coreutils with its ancestor, we offer insights into creating a reliable substitute for critical software and highlight how modern programming features can attract development interest in legacy packages.

</details>


### [322] [Towards Assurance Closure in AI-Native Large-Scale Agile Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07317)
*Ricardo Britto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI原生宣言"愿景，设想大规模敏捷软件开发中人类管理意图、风险和异常，代理执行工程过程。实现这一目标需要"保证闭环"（assurance closure），即系统能建立必须为真的事项、获取并判断证据、保持其有效性并据此限制代理权限。论文在梳理现有机制后，识别出六个残余缺口，提出包含六项能力的架构和四个研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠更好的代码生成无法实现AI原生软件开发愿景，必须建立机器可操作的保证推理体系（assurance reasoning），使系统能自我建立信任边界并据此授权给AI代理。

Method: 分析现有工作（形式化方法、测试、仿真、保证案例、数字孪生、运行时保证），识别六个残余缺口；提出高层架构，包含六项对应能力，构建于共享语义保证层之上；制定四个研究问题。

Result: 识别出使保证推理机器可操作的六个残余缺口，提出六项能力的架构设计，并制定四个研究问题以将该架构转化为可靠的人类在环AI原生研发系统。

Conclusion: 实现AI原生软件研发需要超越代码生成，构建完整的"保证闭环"机制——让系统能够自我论证什么必须为真、如何获得证据、如何评估证据可信度、如何在变更中保持有效性，并以此不确定性为依据约束代理权限，最终实现人类监督下的可信AI研发。

Abstract: The AI-Native Manifesto envisions large-scale agile software development in which humans increasingly govern intent, risk, and exceptions while agents execute more of the engineering process. Realizing that end-state requires more than better code generation: it requires assurance closure, meaning that the system can establish what must be true, determine and obtain appropriate evidence, judge the credibility of that evidence, preserve its validity through change, and use the resulting uncertainty to bound agent authority. Existing work already provides many of the necessary mechanisms across formal methods, testing, simulation, assurance cases, digital twins, and runtime assurance. We identify six residual gaps in making the surrounding assurance reasoning sufficiently machine-operable, propose a high-level architecture with six corresponding capabilities built on a shared semantic assurance layer, and formulate four research questions to turn that architecture into dependable, human-on-the-loop, AI-native R&D.

</details>


### [323] [PACE: Primitive-Aware Code Evolution for Automated Algorithm Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07395)
*Zhuoliang Xie,Ruihao Zheng,Xiang Xu,Genghui Li,Zhengkun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PACE（Primitive-Aware Code Evolution），一种将算法演化从完整程序解耦为可执行算法原语（EAPs）的方法，通过维护动态原语集合并使用基于Thompson采样的原始感知算子实现跨程序代码转移，在四个任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动算法设计通常将算法视为完整不可分割的程序进行演化，这种"整体程序"视角虽然简化了搜索空间，但将有用的局部逻辑与宿主程序紧密耦合，导致有价值的代码片段在整体程序被丢弃时随之消失，且难以评估单个算法组件的贡献。

Method: PACE将局部逻辑从完整程序中解耦，表示为可执行的算法原语（EAPs）作为持久单元；维护一个动态EAP集合实现代码级转移；使用原始感知算子从结构上保证组件的保留和跨程序转移；基于父代相对性能改进采用Thompson采样来指导原语选择，无需额外评估数据集。

Result: 在四个任务上的实验表明，PACE能有效发现具有竞争力的算法，同时从结构上保留有价值的算法组件。

Conclusion: 通过将算法组件从完整程序中解耦并支持跨程序转移，PACE解决了LLM自动算法设计中组件评估困难和代码重用难的问题，为可组合、可复用的自动算法设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based automated algorithm design typically evolves algorithms as complete, indivisible programs. While this whole-program perspective simplifies the search space, it fundamentally couples the useful local logic to its host program. Consequently, valuable code snippets vanish when the overall program is discarded, making it highly difficult to assess the contribution of individual algorithmic components.To address this, we propose Primitive-Aware Code Evolution (PACE), which decouples local logic from complete programs by representing it as persistent units called Executable Algorithmic Primitives (EAPs). To enable code-level transfer, PACE maintains a dynamic set of EAPs. Algorithm evolution is driven by primitive-aware operators that structurally guarantee the retention and cross-program transfer of these components. To evaluate them effectively, PACE leverages Thompson sampling based on parent-relative performance improvements, guiding primitive selection from the set without requiring extra evaluation datasets. Experiments on four tasks demonstrate that PACE effectively discovers competitive algorithms while structurally preserving valuable algorithmic components.

</details>


### [324] [Circuit-Based Program Verification: Sequential Circuits as an Intermediate Representation for Verifying C Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07397)
*Po-Chun Chien,Nian-Ze Lee,Armin Biere,Dirk Beyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit-Based Program Verification (CPV)框架，将C程序翻译为时序电路，利用现成的硬件模型检验器作为后端进行软件验证，在16000多个基准任务上取得了与五种主流软件验证器相当的性能，并展现出互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件验证与硬件验证虽然都致力于对状态转换系统进行推理，但两个社区分别发展了独立的中间表示和验证算法。作者希望利用硬件模型检验技术的成熟优势，通过将软件程序转化为时序电路，从而直接复用硬件验证算法。

Method: CPV是一个模块化框架，将C程序编译为时序电路（将程序的控制流和数据流折叠为整体转换关系），并集成多种现成硬件模型检验器（支持有界模型检验、k-归纳和IC3/PDR等算法）。支持可达性安全性和终止性分析，并将硬件模型检验器发现的反例自动翻译回软件验证见证格式。

Result: 在超过16000个任务的基准套件上进行全面评估，CPV与五种成熟软件验证器相比具有竞争力，并且能够独特地解决其他验证器无法处理的任务，展现出互补优势。

Conclusion: 将时序电路作为软件验证的中间表示是可行且有效的，CPV证明了硬件模型检验技术可以直接应用于软件验证，并能为现有软件验证工具集提供互补能力。

Abstract: Formal verification of software programs and hardware designs shares the common goal of reasoning about state-transition systems, yet the two communities have largely developed separate intermediate representations and verification algorithms. This paper investigates sequential circuits as an intermediate representation for software verification, with the goal of enabling direct application of hardware-model-checking techniques. We present Circuit-Based Program Verification (CPV), a modular framework that translates C programs into sequential circuits and employs off-the-shelf hardware model checkers as backends. Unlike traditional software verifiers, which typically rely on path-based exploration, CPV reasons over sequential circuits, where a program's control and data flows are folded into a monolithic transition relation that can be analyzed as a whole. The framework supports reachability-safety and termination analyses and integrates multiple state-of-the-art hardware model checkers, which together provide access to diverse verification algorithms, including bounded model checking, $k$-induction, and IC3/PDR. Counterexamples found by hardware model checkers are automatically translated back into software-verification witnesses for users to interpret verification results. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation on a benchmark suite of more than 16000 tasks. Our results show that CPV achieved competitive performance against five well-established software verifiers and exhibited complementary strengths by uniquely solving tasks that other verifiers cannot handle.

</details>


### [325] [Taxonomy-Driven Analysis of Open-Source AI Risk Mitigation Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07446)
*Afreen Alam,Evgenija Popchanovska,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Maryan Rizinski,Lubomir T. Chitkushev,Irena Vodenska,Dimitar Trajanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分类学的结构化协议，通过LLM辅助的检索增强生成（RAG）流程，将21个开源LLM评估和安全工具的能力映射到扩展版MIT AI风险缓解与响应分类法的32个子类别中。研究发现工具能力分布高度不均衡，主要集中在技术和运维控制上，而治理、法律监管及金融市场控制方面存在重大缺口，并据此提出了分层风险缓解架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业大规模采用LLM带来了运营、安全和治理风险，但随着生成式AI应用从试点走向生产，人工识别和缓解危害变得难以规模化。现有工具生态系统碎片化，工具通常针对特定工程任务设计，技术表述与治理框架或风险分类不一致，导致难以判断哪些工具应对哪些风险、关键缺口在哪里。

Method: 提出分类学驱动的自动化AI风险缓解协议：1) 使用扩展MIT AI风险缓解与响应分类法作为映射基准；2) 构建LLM辅助的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，从源码和文档中提取工具能力；3) 将21个重要开源工具映射到32个分类子类别；4) 采用多数投票机制，并以三名独立评审员进行可靠性评估（Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509）。

Result: 映射协议在多数投票后达到F1分数75.5%；评审一致性为中等（Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509）；工具能力呈高度偏斜分布，集中在技术和运维控制，治理、法律监管及金融市场控制覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 提供了一个企业AI风险类别与开源缓解能力之间的实用映射，识别了仍需人工监督的领域，并提出结合工具控制与组织和监管流程的分层风险缓解架构。该分类学驱动框架同样适用于专有解决方案。

Abstract: Rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) in enterprise settings has introduced operational, security, and governance risks. As generative AI applications move from pilot to production, manual harm identification and mitigation are becoming difficult to scale. Although many tools support model evaluation, adversarial testing, runtime guardrails, and observability, the tooling landscape remains fragmented. Tools are typically designed for specific engineering tasks and described in technical terms that do not align with governance frameworks or risk taxonomies, making it difficult to determine which tools address which risks and where critical gaps remain. This paper proposes a structured protocol to automate AI risk mitigation through a taxonomy-driven analysis of open-source LLM evaluation and security tools. We map the capabilities of 21 prominent open-source tools to the 32 subcategories of the extended MIT AI Risk Mitigation and Response Taxonomy. An LLM-assisted retrieval-augmented generation pipeline analyzes source code and documentation to extract capabilities for each taxonomy category. Reliability assessment yielded moderate agreement (Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509) among three independent reviewers. The analysis reveals a highly skewed landscape in which tools cluster around technical and operational controls, while governance, legal and regulatory, and financial and market controls remain largely unaddressed. This motivates a layered risk-mitigation architecture combining tool-based controls with organizational and regulatory processes. The mapping protocol achieved an F1 score of 75.5% after majority voting. Overall, the study provides a practical mapping between enterprise AI risk categories and open-source mitigation capabilities, identifies where human oversight remains necessary, and presents a taxonomy-driven framework applicable to open-source and proprietary solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [326] [Auctioning Attention on Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06665)
*Andy Lee,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拍卖机制的社交媒体信息流构建方法，让用户通过竞价来获取其他用户的注意力。该方法系统性考虑了内容生产者、消费者、平台运营商和社会福利，并通过税收政策对具有负面外部性的内容增加成本。实验表明该方法在生产者福利和注意力分配公平性上均优于对比算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体推荐系统存在多方利益冲突：生产者被迫优化算法、消费者暴露于错误信息和极化内容、平台追求用户参与度导致过度消费，同时面临政策制定者和社会的压力。现有方法仅聚焦于推荐系统，缺乏系统性考虑所有利益相关者的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于拍卖的信息流构建机制：(1) 用户通过竞价获取其他用户的注意力；(2) 机制系统性考虑生产者、消费者、平台和社会福利；(3) 引入税收政策增加具有负面外部性内容的成本；(4) 证明该拍卖在预算约束下是弱激励相容的；(5) 通过模拟常见社交网络拓扑和经验观察到的网络进行验证。

Result: 在经验观察网络上，所提出的拍卖机制比对比算法平均提高36.3%的生产者福利；在合成网络上提高31.4%。在所有评估的网络类型中，该方法比基线方法产生更公平的注意力分配。

Conclusion: 所提出的拍卖机制在系统层面解决了注意力分配问题，平衡了不同利益相关者的需求，提供了一种替代传统推荐系统的可行方案。

Abstract: Social media recommendation systems create conflict: content producers, content consumers, platform operators, and social pressures struggle to direct the allocation of attention in their favor while also facing competing societal pressures. Producers may feel pressure to optimize for recommendation algorithms and consumers may be exposed to content with negative externalities such as polarization and misinformation. Platforms aim to maximize user engagement, oftentimes resulting in over consumption from consumers. There is mounting pressure from policymakers and broader society to address these issues. Prior methods for constructing social media feeds have focused on recommendation systems. Instead, we propose a, to our knowledge, novel auction based method for feed construction where users bid for the attention of other users. Our mechanism systematically considers producers, consumers, platform operators, and social welfare. We show that our auction is weakly incentive compatible under budget constraints. To balance between producer and consumer welfare, we introduce a tax policy to the auction to increase the cost of content with negative externalities. Simulations over common social network topologies and an empirically observed network show how different feed algorithms prioritize different stakeholders. Our proposed auction based mechanisms produce on average 36.3% higher producer welfare than comparison algorithms on the empirically observed network and 31.4% higher producer welfare than comparison algorithms on the synthetic networks. Our methods also produce more equitable distributions of attention than baseline methods across all evaluated network types. Our proposed mechanism addresses attention allocation at a systematic level, balancing between the needs of different stakeholders.

</details>


### [327] [Pessimal Elections for Approximately Dominating Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06872)
*Moses Charikar,Prasanna Ramakrishnan,Kangning Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对Condorcet悖论中委员会选举的多数阈值放宽问题，提出一个简单构造（由GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra发现），证明此前关于O(1/ε²)获胜者数量的结果在常数因子内是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Condorcet悖论表明无论选择谁获胜，都可能有多数选民偏好某个落选者。放宽多数阈值（即要求1/2+ε的选民支持而非严格多数）可以规避这一障碍，先前工作已证明O(1/ε²)获胜者足以保证没有输家能击败所有获胜者。本文的核心动机是确定这一上界是否最优，即是否存在下界匹配。

Method: 作者提供了一个简单构造（由GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra发现），通过该构造证明了此前O(1/ε²)的获胜者数量界在常数因子内是紧的，即存在实例使得需要Ω(1/ε²)个获胜者才能满足条件。

Result: 证明了现有的O(1/ε²)委员会规模结果在常数因子内是紧的，即下界与上界匹配，不存在显著改进空间。

Conclusion: 该结果封闭了委员会选举中放宽多数阈值问题的主要复杂度间隙，确认O(1/ε²)获胜者数量在常数因子内是最优的。

Abstract: Condorcet's paradox is a foundational result in social choice theory, showing that no matter which candidate wins an election, a majority of voters may prefer some losing candidate. Worse still, even if the election can choose a committee of $k$ winners, some loser may beat every winner in a majority vote.
  Recent work showed that this obstruction can be sidestepped by relaxing the majority threshold. For all $\varepsilon > 0$, any election can select a committee of $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ winners such that no loser is preferred to every winner by $\frac12 + \varepsilon$ fraction of voters.
  We present a simple construction, found by GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra, which proves that this result is tight up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [328] [Ex-Post Equilibria: Structure and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07025)
*Francesco Giordano,Julien Grand-Clément,Christian Kroer*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究同时行动博弈中参数不确定条件下的事后均衡（EPE），将其与现有稳健均衡概念进行比较，证明EPE由单调性和集合一致性两个博弈论性质完全刻画，并引入最优近似事后均衡概念，针对零和博弈与凹势博弈两类博弈给出了计算复杂度结果及基于辅助极小极大公式的计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在博弈论中，参数不确定性问题普遍存在，而现有稳健均衡概念（如Bayes均衡、minimax均衡等）在刻画事后均衡方面存在局限。EPE作为一种自然且重要的解概念，缺乏系统的理论基础和计算方法的深入研究，因此需要为其建立一般性基础并解决计算问题。

Method: 首先通过公理化方法比较EPE与现有稳健均衡概念，证明EPE可由单调性和集合一致性两个性质完全刻画；其次针对EPE可能不存在的情况，引入最优近似事后均衡（通过最小化次优程度允许近似最优响应）；最后针对零和博弈和凹势博弈两类重要博弈，通过辅助极小极大公式设计计算方法，并分析计算复杂度。

Result: 给出了EPE的公理化刻画（单调性和集合一致性是充分必要条件）；证明了在某些情形下EPE可能不存在，因此最优近似EPE是必要的；对零和博弈和凹势博弈给出了若干计算硬度结果，并提出了基于辅助极小极大公式的通用计算框架。

Conclusion: 本文为参数不确定条件下的EPE建立了坚实的理论基础，阐明了其与现有稳健解概念的关系，并提供了有效的计算方法框架，同时揭示了计算EPE和最优近似EPE在重要博弈类别中的计算复杂性边界。

Abstract: This paper studies ex-post equilibria (EPEs) in simultaneous-move games with parameter uncertainty. We first compare EPEs with existing notions of robust equilibrium and provide a general foundation for EPEs as a solution concept for games with parameter uncertainty, and we show that EPEs are fully characterized by two game-theoretic properties (monotonicity and set-consistency). Since ex-post equilibria may fail to exist, we introduce the notion of an optimal approximate ex-post equilibrium, in which players adopt approximate best responses while minimizing the degree of suboptimality. We study the problem of computing EPEs and optimal approximate EPEs, focusing on two important classes of games: zero-sum games and concave potential games. We provide several hardness results, as well as a general class of computational approaches based on auxiliary minimax formulations.

</details>


### [329] [Algorithmic Threshold Optimization: Quantitative Modeling of Multiplier Distributions in Crash Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07103)
*Sourish Sarkar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对流行赌场游戏Crash提出了一种优化算法，旨在最小化赌场有保障的正收益，同时最大化每轮中获胜玩家数量。该算法确定一个最优停止乘数，以平衡赌场与玩家的利益，且不受玩家数量或投资金额变化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Crash是一款融合策略决策、概率和计算分析的多人赌场游戏。由于是多玩家游戏，玩家在不同时间点随机投入金额，使得任何单个玩家在游戏开始前难以判断自己的投资相对高低，从而难以评估实际承担的风险。因此需要一种算法来帮助平衡赌场和玩家的利益。

Method: 提出一种优化算法，核心目标是确定一个最优停止乘数，该乘数同时兼顾赌场与玩家的利益。算法通过优化策略在最小化赌场有保障正收益的同时最大化获胜玩家数量。算法对玩家数量变化和投资金额变化具有不变性，并进一步分析了算法的计算复杂度和停止乘数统计特性。

Result: 算法不受玩家数量或投资金额变化的影响（尽管实践中赌场通常对投资设定固定范围限制）。此外，该算法能帮助专业玩家在投资金额和可接受风险方面做出更明智的决策。论文还给出了算法的计算复杂度分析及停止乘数统计学特征。

Conclusion: 该优化算法为Crash游戏提供了一种同时考虑赌场与玩家利益的平衡方案，不仅对赌场具有重要意义，还能辅助专业玩家进行更理性的投资决策。算法的鲁棒性（不受玩家数量及投资金额影响）和可分析的计算复杂度使其具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Crash is a widely played casino game that blends strategic decision-making, probability, and computational analysis, particularly from the perspective of the house. This paper presents an optimization algorithm aimed at minimizing the casino's guaranteed positive earnings while simultaneously maximizing the number of players who win some amount during a given round. Since Crash is inherently a multiplayer game, players place random investment amounts at different points in time, which makes it difficult for any individual player to judge, before the round even begins, whether their intended investment is comparatively high or low, and consequently, how much risk they are actually taking on. The core objective of the proposed algorithm is to determine an optimal stopping multiplier that balances both the casino's and the players' interests. Notably, the algorithm remains unaffected by changes in the number of players or by variation in investment amounts, although in practice casinos typically impose a fixed range on permissible investments. Beyond this, the algorithm can help professional players make more informed decisions about how much to invest and how much risk they are comfortable accepting. We further analyze the algorithm's computational complexity and its stopping-multiplier statistics to give a fuller picture of how it behaves in practice.

</details>


### [330] [Analyzing the Interaction of Optimal Strategies in Mean-Payoff Bidding Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07383)
*Shaull Almagor,Guy Avni,Julian Ewaied*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中所有智能体都采用对抗性假设（即假设其他智能体是敌对的）进行策略设计时，实际交互中会产生什么样的行为。以双人拍卖竞价游戏（bidding game）为研究对象，分析当双方都针对最坏情况优化策略时产生的博弈路径，证明在受限条件下该路径是最终周期的，并提出计算玩家效用的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统设计中，常见的假设是其他智能体是敌对的，这样可以获得最强保障。但当所有智能体都采用对抗性假设进行设计时，实际交互并不真对抗（如双方都防守时无人进攻）。理解这种设定下系统实际涌现的行为很重要，但分析智能体间的交互在数学和算法上都极具挑战性。

Method: 聚焦于图上的双人竞价博弈（bidding game）：令牌置于顶点上，每回合通过拍卖决定哪个智能体移动令牌，生成无限路径从而决定效用。采用mean-payoff目标（路径上奖励的极限平均）。分析每个智能体都采用针对敌手最优化的策略时的博弈结果，考虑两种已知的最优策略显式构造。技术难点来自竞价博弈的无限配置及其复杂动力学，本文在若干限制条件下证明产生的博弈路径最终是周期的，并开发计算玩家效用的算法。

Result: 在一些限制条件下，证明了当双方都采用对抗最优策略时产生的博弈路径最终呈现周期性（ultimately periodic），并开发了相应的算法来计算在该路径上各玩家的效用。

Conclusion: 本文为"所有智能体采用对抗性假设设计时实际产生的行为"这一基本问题提供了理论分析，在竞价博弈这一具体设定下揭示了系统的长期行为具有周期性结构，为理解和预测此类多智能体系统的涌现行为提供了理论工具和计算方法。

Abstract: A common assumption when designing an agent in a multi-agent system is that the other agents behave adversarially. This allows a designer to obtain the strongest guarantees when they have no control over nor knowledge about the other agents' behavior. However, when all agents are designed under this adversarial assumption, their actual interaction is not adversarial (e.g., when all players play defensively, no player actually attacks). In such settings, we would like to know what behavior arises in the multi-agent system. However, analyzing the interaction among agents is notoriously challenging, both mathematically and algorithmically. In this paper, we provide such an analysis, focusing on bidding games, played by two agents on a graph as follows. A token is placed on a vertex, and in each turn an auction (bidding) determines which agent moves the token, thus generating an infinite path that determines the agents' utilities. We consider mean-payoff objectives; each vertex is associated with a reward for each player, and the utility in an infinite play is the limit average of the rewards. We analyze the play that is generated when each agent follows a strategy that optimizes against an adversary, and consider the two known explicit constructions of optimal strategies. The technical challenge stems from the infinitely-many configurations of a bidding game and their complicated dynamics. We show that, under some restrictions, the generated play is ultimately periodic, and develop algorithms to compute the players' utilities in it.

</details>


### [331] [Bayesian Fair Division: Truthfulness in Picking Sequence with Correlated Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07414)
*Xiaolin Bu,Biaoshuai Tao*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯公平分配模型中序列分配机制的真实性。针对两个代理人的情况，论文精确刻画了使代理人说真话成为贝叶斯纳什均衡的"估值粗略一致"程度；但证明该真实性无法推广到两个以上代理人的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知序列分配机制（如轮转法）在完全信息下不具真实性，原因在于代理人对他人估值有完全知识，且不同物品对各代理人的价值差异大。自然的问题是：当代理人只拥有部分信息且估值大致一致时，机制是否会更真实？

Method: 在贝叶斯公平分配模型下进行理论分析。针对两个代理人的情形，精确刻画了估值正相关性（粗略一致性）的条件，证明在此条件下说真话构成贝叶斯纳什均衡；随后分析两个以上代理人的情形，揭示新的操纵类型。

Result: 两个代理人且估值正相关时，序列分配机制下说真话为贝叶斯纳什均衡；但该真实性结论不适用于两个以上代理人，且发现了不同于以往延迟选高价值低竞争物品的新型操纵方式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了序列分配机制真实性的根本局限：虽然部分信息与估值一致性在双代理人场景下能恢复真实性，但多代理人场景中真实性的缺失是本质性的，无法通过类似假设修复。

Abstract: Sequential allocation mechanisms contain a class of widely studied mechanisms (e.g., round-robin) in the fair division of indivisible goods, where agents take turns picking items in a predefined picking order. It is known that the sequential allocation mechanisms are not truthful: when an agent's most preferred item is not valued by others, the agent may manipulate the mechanism by choosing to defer picking that item and instead competing for another slightly less preferred item that is valued by others. Two underlying reasons are that each agent has perfect knowledge of the others' valuations, and each item's value to each agent can differ significantly. Will the mechanism be more truthful when each agent only has partial information about the others' valuations, which are known to be roughly consistent? This naturally motivates the study of the Bayesian fair division model.
  In this paper, we answer this question affirmatively for two agents. Under the Bayesian model, we precisely characterize the extent of this ``rough consistency'' that incentivizes agents' truth-telling. In particular, we show that for the case of two agents, when the valuations are positively correlated, truth-telling forms a Bayesian Nash equilibrium under the sequential allocation mechanisms. However, we show that truthfulness fails to extend to the setting with more than two agents. For more than two agents, we reveal a new type of manipulation that is different from the above-mentioned manipulation that defers a highly valued but less competitive item. Our result reveals a fundamental limitation on the truthfulness of sequential mechanisms.

</details>
